136: function validateConfig(config) {

163:     }
164:     if (processIds.has(process.id)) {
165:       throw new RangeError(`duplicate process: ${process.id}`);

167:     processIds.add(process.id);
168: 
169:     if (!Number.isInteger(process.order) || process.order < 1) {
170:       throw new RangeError(`process ${process.id} needs a positive integer order`);

172:     if (orders.has(process.order)) {
173:       throw new RangeError(`duplicate process order: ${process.order}`);

175:     orders.add(process.order);

202: /**
203:  * Pure management rules for the Dash Supplement Factory.
204:  *
205:  * The player takes empty pots from an unlimited intake, drags each one into the
206:  * station for its current numbered process, and moves it on before the ready
207:  * window expires. Stations process four pots at once. After all twelve
208:  * processes, a pot goes to the bottler and produces 20 one-second vials.
209:  *
210:  * The core has no DOM or wall clock. The host reports elapsed time with
211:  * `advance(deltaMs)` and receives every completion, spoil, and bottling event.
212:  */
213: export class DashSupplementFactory {
214:   constructor({ config = {} } = {}) {
215:     this.config = { ...DEFAULT_CONFIG, ...config };
216:     validateConfig(this.config);
217: 
218:     this.stations = this.config.stations.map((station) => ({ ...station }));
219:     this.processes = this.config.processes
220:       .map((process) => ({ ...process }))
221:       .sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order);
222: 
223:     this._pots = [];
224:     this._nextPotId = 1;
225:     this._elapsedMs = 0;
226:     this._vialsProduced = 0;
227:     this._potsBottled = 0;
228:     this._potsSpoiled = 0;
229:   }
230: 
231:   get pots() {
232:     return this._pots.map((pot) => ({ ...pot }));
233:   }
234: 
235:   get activePots() {
236:     return this._pots
237:       .filter((pot) => pot.location !== 'exited')
238:       .map((pot) => ({ ...pot }));
239:   }
240: 
241:   get intakePots() {
242:     return this.#potsAt('intake');
243:   }
244: 
245:   get elapsedMs() {
246:     return this._elapsedMs;
247:   }
248: 
249:   get vialsProduced() {
250:     return this._vialsProduced;
251:   }
252: 
253:   get secondsProduced() {
254:     return this._vialsProduced * this.config.secondsPerVial;
255:   }
256: 
257:   get potsBottled() {
258:     return this._potsBottled;
259:   }
260: 
261:   get potsSpoiled() {
262:     return this._potsSpoiled;
263:   }
264: 
265:   get stationCapacity() {
266:     return this.config.stationCapacity;
267:   }
268: 
269:   get bottlerCapacity() {
270:     return this.config.bottlerCapacity;
271:   }
272: 
273:   /** Active pots at one station, the intake, or the bottler. */
274:   getPotsAt(location) {
275:     return this.#potsAt(location);
276:   }
277: 
278:   /** Number of occupied slots per station, plus the bottler. */
279:   get occupancy() {
280:     const result = {};
281: 
282:     for (const station of this.stations) {
283:       result[station.id] = this.#potsAt(station.id).length;
284:     }
285:     result.bottler = this.#potsAt('bottler').length;
286: 
287:     return result;
288:   }
289: 
290:   /** The process that is running or awaiting movement for a pot, if any. */
291:   processFor(potId) {
292:     const pot = this._pots.find((candidate) => candidate.id === potId);
293: 
294:     if (!pot || pot.stepIndex >= this.processes.length) {
295:       return null;
296:     }
297:     return clone(this.processes[pot.stepIndex]);
298:   }
299: 
300:   /** Add one empty pot to the intake. There is no limit. */
301:   takePot() {
302:     const pot = {
303:       id: this._nextPotId,
304:       stepIndex: 0,
305:       location: 'intake',
306:       status: POT_STATUS.IDLE,
307:       progressMs: 0,
308:       windowMsLeft: 0,
309:       spoiled: false,
310:       bottled: false,
311:     };
312:     this._nextPotId += 1;
313:     this._pots.push(pot);
314: 
315:     return {
316:       type: 'pot-taken',
317:       potId: pot.id,
318:       pot: { ...pot },
319:       intake: this.#potsAt('intake').length,
320:     };
321:   }
322: 
323:   /**
324:    * Drag a pot into a station or the bottler.
325:    *
326:    * A pot waiting at the intake or ready at a station can move. Working pots
327:    * are rejected with `pot-busy`; a wrong station leaves the pot where it was.
328:    */
329:   dropPot(potId, targetId) {
330:     const pot = this._pots.find((candidate) => candidate.id === potId);
331: 
332:     if (!pot) {
333:       return this.#rejected('unknown-pot');
334:     }
335:     if (pot.location === 'exited' || pot.status === POT_STATUS.SPOILED) {
336:       return this.#rejected('pot-gone');
337:     }
338:     if (
339:       pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING ||
340:       pot.status === POT_STATUS.QUEUED ||
341:       pot.status === POT_STATUS.BOTTLING
342:     ) {
343:       return this.#rejected('pot-busy');
344:     }
345: 
346:     if (targetId === 'bottler') {
347:       return this.#dropAtBottler(pot);
348:     }
349: 
350:     const station = this.stations.find((candidate) => candidate.id === targetId);
351: 
352:     if (!station) {
353:       return this.#rejected('unknown-station');
354:     }
355:     if (pot.location === targetId) {
356:       return this.#rejected('already-there');
357:     }
358: 
359:     const step = this.processes[pot.stepIndex];
360: 
361:     if (!step) {
362:       return this.#rejected('ready-for-bottler');
363:     }
364:     if (step.stationId !== targetId) {
365:       return this.#rejected('wrong-station', {
366:         expectedStationId: step.stationId,
367:         process: clone(step),
368:       });
369:     }
370:     if (this.#potsAt(targetId).length >= this.config.stationCapacity) {
371:       return this.#rejected('station-full');
372:     }
373: 
374:     const from = pot.location;
375:     pot.location = targetId;
376:     pot.status = POT_STATUS.WORKING;
377:     pot.progressMs = 0;
378:     pot.windowMsLeft = 0;
379: 
380:     return {
381:       type: 'pot-dropped',
382:       potId: pot.id,
383:       from,
384:       to: targetId,
385:       status: pot.status,
386:       stepIndex: pot.stepIndex,
387:       step: clone(step),
388:     };
389:   }
390: 
391:   /**
392:    * Advance every working, ready, and bottling pot by `deltaMs`.
393:    *
394:    * Returns a tick with an ordered `events` array. The array can contain
395:    * process completions, spoiled pots, and bottled pots in the same tick.
396:    */
397:   advance(deltaMs) {
398:     if (!Number.isFinite(deltaMs) || deltaMs <= 0) {
399:       return this.#tick(0, []);
400:     }
401: 
402:     this._elapsedMs += deltaMs;
403:     const events = [];
404: 
405:     for (const pot of this._pots) {
406:       if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING) {
407:         const step = this.processes[pot.stepIndex];
408: 
409:         if (!step) {
410:           continue;
411:         }
412: 
413:         const completedIndex = pot.stepIndex;
414:         pot.progressMs += deltaMs;
415: 
416:         if (pot.progressMs >= step.durationMs) {
417:           const overflow = pot.progressMs - step.durationMs;
418:           pot.progressMs = step.durationMs;
419:           pot.status = POT_STATUS.READY;
420:           pot.windowMsLeft = step.windowMs;
421:           pot.stepIndex += 1;
422:           events.push({
423:             type: 'process-complete',
424:             potId: pot.id,
425:             stationId: pot.location,
426:             stepIndex: completedIndex,
427:             step: clone(step),
428:             nextStep: clone(this.processes[pot.stepIndex] ?? null),
429:             windowMsLeft: pot.windowMsLeft,
430:           });
431: 
432:           if (overflow > 0) {
433:             pot.windowMsLeft -= overflow;
434: 
435:             if (pot.windowMsLeft <= 0) {
436:               this.#spoil(pot, events, completedIndex);
437:             }
438:           }
439:         }
440:       } else if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.READY) {
441:         pot.windowMsLeft -= deltaMs;
442: 
443:         if (pot.windowMsLeft <= 0) {
444:           this.#spoil(pot, events, Math.max(0, pot.stepIndex - 1));
445:         }
446:       }
447:     }
448: 
449:     // The bottler empties one pot at a time. Anything else in the bottler
450:     // waits as a queue, so a large delta can finish several pots in order.
451:     let bottlingRemaining = deltaMs;
452: 
453:     while (bottlingRemaining > 0) {
454:       const active = this._pots.find(
455:         (candidate) =>
456:           candidate.location === 'bottler' &&
457:           candidate.status === POT_STATUS.BOTTLING,
458:       );
459: 
460:       if (!active) {
461:         break;
462:       }
463: 
464:       const needed = this.config.bottlingMs - active.progressMs;
465: 
466:       if (bottlingRemaining < needed) {
467:         active.progressMs += bottlingRemaining;
468:         break;
469:       }
470: 
471:       bottlingRemaining -= needed;
472:       active.progressMs = this.config.bottlingMs;
473:       active.status = POT_STATUS.DONE;
474:       active.bottled = true;
475:       active.location = 'exited';
476:       this._vialsProduced += this.config.vialsPerPot;
477:       this._potsBottled += 1;
478:       events.push({
479:         type: 'pot-bottled',
480:         potId: active.id,
481:         vials: this.config.vialsPerPot,
482:         seconds: this.config.vialsPerPot * this.config.secondsPerVial,
483:         vialsProduced: this._vialsProduced,
484:         secondsProduced: this.secondsProduced,
485:       });
486: 
487:       this.#promoteQueuedPot(events);
488:     }
489: 
490:     return this.#tick(deltaMs, events);
491:   }
492: 
493:   /** Remove all produced vials from the module and return the count. */
494:   takeVials() {
495:     const vials = this._vialsProduced;
496:     this._vialsProduced = 0;
497:     return vials;
498:   }
499: 
500:   /** Remove all produced vials and return their total speed duration. */
501:   takeSeconds() {
502:     return this.takeVials() * this.config.secondsPerVial;
503:   }
504: 
505:   /** Backwards-compatible aliases for hosts that still call them bottles. */
506:   takeBottles() {
507:     return this.takeVials();
508:   }
509: 
510:   #dropAtBottler(pot) {
511:     if (pot.stepIndex < this.processes.length) {
512:       return this.#rejected('processes-remaining', {
513:         process: clone(this.processes[pot.stepIndex]),
514:       });
515:     }
516:     if (this.#potsAt('bottler').length >= this.config.bottlerCapacity) {
517:       return this.#rejected('bottler-full');
518:     }
519: 
520:     const from = pot.location;
521:     const bottlerBusy = this._pots.some(
522:       (candidate) =>
523:         candidate.location === 'bottler' &&
524:         candidate.status === POT_STATUS.BOTTLING,
525:     );
526: 
527:     pot.location = 'bottler';
528:     pot.status = bottlerBusy ? POT_STATUS.QUEUED : POT_STATUS.BOTTLING;
529:     pot.progressMs = 0;
530:     pot.windowMsLeft = 0;
531: 
532:     return {
533:       type: 'pot-dropped',
534:       potId: pot.id,
535:       from,
536:       to: 'bottler',
537:       status: pot.status,
538:       stepIndex: pot.stepIndex,
539:       step: null,
540:     };
541:   }
542: 
543:   /** Start the next waiting pot in the bottler, if any. */
544:   #promoteQueuedPot(events) {
545:     const next = this._pots.find(
546:       (candidate) =>
547:         candidate.location === 'bottler' &&
548:         candidate.status === POT_STATUS.QUEUED,
549:     );
550: 
551:     if (!next) {
552:       return null;
553:     }
554: 
555:     next.status = POT_STATUS.BOTTLING;
556:     next.progressMs = 0;
557:     events.push({
558:       type: 'bottling-started',
559:       potId: next.id,
560:     });
561: 
562:     return next;
563:   }
564: 
565:   #spoil(pot, events, stepIndex = pot.stepIndex) {
566:     const stationId = pot.location;
567: 
568:     pot.status = POT_STATUS.SPOILED;
569:     pot.location = 'exited';
570:     pot.spoiled = true;
571:     pot.windowMsLeft = 0;
572:     pot.progressMs = 0;
573:     this._potsSpoiled += 1;
574:     events.push({
575:       type: 'pot-spoiled',
576:       potId: pot.id,
577:       stationId,
578:       stepIndex,
579:       reason: 'window',
580:     });
581:   }
582: 
583:   #potsAt(location) {
584:     return this._pots
585:       .filter((pot) => pot.location === location)
586:       .map((pot) => ({ ...pot }));
587:   }
588: 
589:   #tick(deltaMs, events) {
590:     return {
591:       type: 'tick',
592:       deltaMs,
593:       elapsedMs: this._elapsedMs,
594:       events,
595:       activePots: this._pots.filter((pot) => pot.location !== 'exited').length,
596:       vialsProduced: this._vialsProduced,
597:     };
598:   }
599: 
600:   #rejected(reason, extra = {}) {
601:     return {
602:       type: 'rejected',
603:       reason,
604:       ...extra,
605:     };
606:   }
607: }

### Core changes

Add `POT_STATUS.QUEUED = 'queued'`.

In `#dropAtBottler`:
- after capacity check, from = pot.location; pot.location='bottler'; progress=0; window=0.
- if no other pot at bottler with status BOTTLING, status=BOTTLING; else status=QUEUED.
- event includes `status`.

But careful: if active bottling pot exists, new pot queued. If no active (all queued? impossible because when one completes it promotes next within same tick), start.

Advance:
- Find the active bottling pot: `const active = this._pots.find(p => p.location==='bottler' && p.status===BOTTLING)`.
- If active, progress only it by deltaMs. On completion: award vials, status DONE, location EXITED, bottled true, potsBottled++, event pot-bottled. Then promote the next queued pot (oldest by id or array order): set status BOTTLING, progress 0, and push event `bottling-started` with potId. Also leftover overflow? For correctness with large delta, we could carry overflow to next pot. The previous implementation didn't cascade but sequential now should ideally use overflow across pots. Deterministic and nicer: loop with remaining delta:
```
let remaining = deltaMs;
while (remaining > 0) {
  const active = find active bottling;
  if (!active) break;
  const needed = bottlingMs - active.progressMs;
  if (remaining < needed) { active.progressMs += remaining; remaining = 0; break; }
  remaining -= needed;
  complete active...
  promote next...
}
```
But be careful events ordering with other station processing in same tick. Current loop iterates all pots; bottler handling separate. We can handle bottler after station loop using the while. Also if delta huge (e.g., advance(60000)) it completes all sequential pots; good deterministic.

- For queued pots, nothing.

Promotion event: maybe not necessary for UI; the UI re-renders each frame? render only on events, but updateLiveUi can read status. When active completes, pot-bottled event triggers render; queued pot now becomes active with status BOTTLING, and UI will style it. But `render()` is called on events, so it will rebuild and show. If promotion happens with no completion event (e.g., first drop), dropPot returns and UI renders. Fine. Add `bottling-started` event anyway? Could help smoke tests. Let's emit it for clarity.

Also `#tick` events order: process events then bottling events. Fine.

Need `bottlerActive` getter maybe for UI? We can derive from pots.

Config `bottlerCapacity` still 4.

Also timing: `bottlingMs` 10000. Vials incremental? If we award vials at end, UI can show vials streaming during drain based on progress thresholds; bank counter only updates at completion. That's acceptable: "in sequence as the pots get empty" visual. But the displayed VIALS stat would jump from 0 to 20 at end. Could be fine. Yet to make belt meaningful, vials are visual only until end. The smoke test earlier verified vials stat remains 0 during bottling; keep that. User didn't ask to change banking.

Actually, maybe better: award vials progressively as emitted. Then VIALS stat counts up during drain (2/s). This matches "in sequence as the pots get empty" and gives feedback. 190: /* ---------- Pots ---------- */
191: 
192: function findPot(potId) {
193:   return core.pots.find((pot) => pot.id === potId) ?? null;
194: }
195: 
196: function potFill(pot) {
197:   if (pot.location === 'intake') {
198:     return 0;
199:   }
200:   if (pot.location === 'bottler') {
201:     // The pot visibly drains as the bottler pulls its contents out.
202:     const remaining = 1 - pot.progressMs / core.config.bottlingMs;
203:     return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, remaining)) * 100;
204:   }
205: 
206:   const total = processByIndex.length;
207:   let completed = Math.min(pot.stepIndex, total);
208:   let fraction = 0;
209: 
210:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING) {
211:     const process = processByIndex[pot.stepIndex];
212:     fraction = process
213:       ? Math.min(1, pot.progressMs / process.durationMs)
214:       : 0;
215:   }
216: 
217:   return Math.min(100, ((completed + fraction) / total) * 100);
218: }
219: 
220: function potLiquid(pot) {
221:   if (pot.location === 'intake') {
222:     return '#484334';
223:   }
224:   if (pot.location === 'bottler') {
225:     return LIQUID.bottler;
226:   }
227: 
228:   const index =
229:     pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING
230:       ? pot.stepIndex
231:       : Math.max(0, pot.stepIndex - 1);
232:   const process = processByIndex[Math.min(index, processByIndex.length - 1)];
233: 
234:   return LIQUID[process?.stationId] ?? '#8d8470';
235: }
236: 
237: function potBadge(pot) {
238:   if (pot.location === 'intake') {
239:     return '○';
240:   }
241:   if (pot.location === 'bottler') {
242:     return 'BTL';
243:   }
244:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING) {
245:     return String(processByIndex[pot.stepIndex]?.order ?? '·');
246:   }
247: 
248:   const next = processByIndex[pot.stepIndex];
249:   return next ? String(next.order) : 'BTL';
250: }
251: 
252: function potTitle(pot) {
253:   if (pot.location === 'intake') {
254:     return `Pot #${pot.id} — empty, drag it to the mill`;
255:   }
256:   if (pot.location === 'bottler') {
257:     const secondsLeft = Math.max(
258:       0,
259:       (core.config.bottlingMs - pot.progressMs) / 1000,
260:     );
261:     return `Pot #${pot.id} — emptying into vials (${secondsLeft.toFixed(
262:       1,
263:     )}s left)`;
264:   }
265:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING) {
266:     const process = processByIndex[pot.stepIndex];
267:     return `Pot #${pot.id} — process ${process.order} ${process.name} in progress`;
268:   }
269: 
270:   const next = processByIndex[pot.stepIndex];
271:   return next
272:     ? `Pot #${pot.id} — ready, next process ${next.order} at ${stationMeta.get(next.stationId).short}`
273:     : `Pot #${pot.id} — ready for the bottler`;
274: }
275: 
276: function isDraggable(pot) {
277:   return pot.location === 'intake' || pot.status === POT_STATUS.READY;
278: }
279: 
280: function meterDetails(pot) {
281:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING) {
282:     const process = processByIndex[pot.stepIndex];
283:     return {
284:       ratio: process ? Math.min(1, pot.progressMs / process.durationMs) : 0,
285:       color: '#96d05c',
286:       mode: 'working',
287:     };
288:   }
289: 
290:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.BOTTLING) {
291:     return {
292:       ratio: Math.min(1, pot.progressMs / core.config.bottlingMs),
293:       color: '#f2c94c',
294:       mode: 'bottling',
295:     };
296:   }
297: 
298:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.READY) {
299:     const process = processByIndex[Math.max(0, pot.stepIndex - 1)];
300:     const ratio = process
301:       ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, pot.windowMsLeft / process.windowMs))
302:       : 0;
303: 
304:     return {
305:       ratio,
306:       color: ratio <= 0.25 ? '#d64545' : ratio <= 0.5 ? '#dd9d34' : '#f2c94c',
307:       mode: 'ready',
308:     };
309:   }
310: 
311:   return { ratio: 0, color: '#96d05c', mode: 'idle' };
312: }
313: 
314: function setPotMeter(el, pot) {
315:   const meter = meterDetails(pot);
316:   el.style.setProperty('--meter', `${Math.round(meter.ratio * 100)}%`);
317:   el.style.setProperty('--meter-color', meter.color);
318:   el.classList.toggle(
319:     'is-warning',
320:     meter.mode === 'ready' && meter.ratio <= 0.5 && meter.ratio > 0.25,
321:   );
322:   el.classList.toggle(
323:     'is-urgent',
324:     meter.mode === 'ready' && meter.ratio <= 0.25,
325:   );
326: }
327: 
328: function createPotElement(pot) {
329:   const el = document.createElement('button');
330:   el.type = 'button';
331:   el.className = 'pot';
332:   el.dataset.potId = String(pot.id);
333:   el.title = potTitle(pot);
334:   el.setAttribute('aria-label', potTitle(pot));
335: 
336:   if (pot.location === 'intake') {
337:     el.classList.add('is-empty');
338:   }
339:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING) {
340:     el.classList.add('is-working');
341:   }
342:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.READY) {
343:     el.classList.add('is-ready');
344:   }
345:   if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.BOTTLING) {
346:     el.classList.add('is-bottling');
347:   }
348:   if (isDraggable(pot)) {
349:     el.classList.add('is-draggable');
350:   }
351: 
352:   el.style.setProperty('--fill', `${potFill(pot)}%`);
353:   el.style.setProperty('--liquid', potLiquid(pot));
354: 
355:   el.innerHTML = `
356:     <span class="pot__badge">${potBadge(pot)}</span>
357:     <span class="pot__lid"></span>
358:     <span class="pot__body"><span class="pot__liquid"></span></span>
359:     <span class="pot__meter"><span class="pot__meter-fill"></span></span>
360:   `;
361: 
362:   setPotMeter(el, pot);
363:   return el;
364: }

455: function stationLabel(id) {
456:   return stationViews.get(id)?.label ?? (id === 'bottler' ? 'BOTTLER' : id);
457: }
458: 
459: function nextDestination(pot) {
460:   if (pot.location === 'bottler') {
461:     return null;
462:   }
463: 
464:   let nextIndex;
465: 
466:   if (pot.location === 'intake') {
467:     nextIndex = 0;
468:   } else if (pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING) {
469:     nextIndex = pot.stepIndex + 1;
470:   } else {
471:     nextIndex = pot.stepIndex;
472:   }
473: 
474:   const nextProcess = processByIndex[nextIndex] ?? null;
475:   return {
476:     toId: nextProcess ? nextProcess.stationId : 'bottler',
477:     nextIndex,
478:     process: nextProcess,
479:   };
480: }

495:         : `Pot #${ready.id} is ready at ${stationLabel(

554: /* ---------- Arrows ---------- */
555: 
556: function svgElement(name) {
557:   return document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', name);
558: }
559: 
560: function nodeRect(id, canvasRect) {
561:   const el = id === 'intake' ? ui.intake : stationViews.get(id)?.card;
562:   if (!el) {
563:     return null;
564:   }
565: 
566:   const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
567:   return {
568:     left: rect.left - canvasRect.left,
569:     top: rect.top - canvasRect.top,
570:     width: rect.width,
571:     height: rect.height,
572:   };
573: }
574: 
575: function centerOf(rect) {
576:   return {
577:     x: rect.left + rect.width / 2,
578:     y: rect.top + rect.height / 2,
579:   };
580: }
581: 
582: function edgePoint(rect, toward, pad = 7) {
583:   const center = centerOf(rect);
584:   const dx = toward.x - center.x;
585:   const dy = toward.y - center.y;
586:   const halfW = rect.width / 2 + pad;
587:   const halfH = rect.height / 2 + pad;
588: 
589:   if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) {
590:     return center;
591:   }
592: 
593:   const scaleX = dx === 0 ? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY : halfW / Math.abs(dx);
594:   const scaleY = dy === 0 ? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY : halfH / Math.abs(dy);
595:   const scale = Math.min(scaleX, scaleY);
596: 
597:   return {
598:     x: center.x + dx * scale,
599:     y: center.y + dy * scale,
600:   };
601: }
602: 
603: function renderArrows() {
604:   const canvasRect = ui.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
605:   const width = Math.max(1, Math.round(canvasRect.width));
606:   const height = Math.max(1, Math.round(canvasRect.height));
607: 
608:   ui.arrows.setAttribute('viewBox', `0 0 ${width} ${height}`);
609:   ui.arrows.replaceChildren();
610: 
611:   const defs = svgElement('defs');
612:   defs.innerHTML = `
613:     <marker id="arrow-dim" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8" refY="5"
614:       markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" orient="auto-start-reverse">
615:       <path d="M0 0 10 5 0 10z" fill="#6d6855"></path>
616:     </marker>
617:     <marker id="arrow-gold" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="8" refY="5"
618:       markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" orient="auto-start-reverse">
619:       <path d="M0 0 10 5 0 10z" fill="#f2c94c"></path>
620:     </marker>
621:   `;
622:   ui.arrows.append(defs);
623: 
624:   const groups = new Map();
625: 
626:   for (const pot of core.activePots) {
627:     const destination = nextDestination(pot);
628:     if (!destination || destination.toId === pot.location) {
629:       continue;
630:     }
631: 
632:     const key = `${pot.location}->${destination.toId}`;
633:     let group = groups.get(key);
634: 
635:     if (!group) {
636:       group = {
637:         fromId: pot.location,
638:         toId: destination.toId,
639:         orders: new Set(),
640:         ready: false,
641:         count: 0,
642:       };
643:       groups.set(key, group);
644:     }
645: 
646:     group.count += 1;
647:     group.ready =
648:       group.ready ||
649:       pot.status === POT_STATUS.READY ||
650:       pot.location === 'intake';
651:     group.orders.add(destination.process?.order ?? 'BTL');
652:   }
653: 
654:   if (groups.size === 0 && core.activePots.length === 0) {
655:     groups.set('intake->mill', {
656:       fromId: 'intake',
657:       toId: 'mill',
658:       orders: new Set([1]),
659:       ready: false,
660:       count: 0,
661:     });
662:   }
663: 
664:   for (const group of groups.values()) {
665:     const fromRect = nodeRect(group.fromId, canvasRect);
666:     const toRect = nodeRect(group.toId, canvasRect);
667: 
668:     if (!fromRect || !toRect) {
669:       continue;
670:     }
671: 
672:     const start = edgePoint(fromRect, centerOf(toRect));
673:     const end = edgePoint(toRect, centerOf(fromRect));
674:     const dx = end.x - start.x;
675:     const dy = end.y - start.y;
676:     const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
677:     const fromIndex = FLOW_INDEX[group.fromId] ?? 0;
678:     const toIndex = FLOW_INDEX[group.toId] ?? 0;
679:     const direction = fromIndex <= toIndex ? 1 : -1;
680:     const bend = Math.min(54, distance * 0.15) * direction;
681:     const normalX = -dy / distance;
682:     const normalY = dx / distance;
683:     const controlX = (start.x + end.x) / 2 + normalX * bend;
684:     const controlY = (start.y + end.y) / 2 + normalY * bend;
685: 
686:     const path = svgElement('path');
687:     path.setAttribute(
688:       'd',
689:       `M ${start.x} ${start.y} Q ${controlX} ${controlY} ${end.x} ${end.y}`,
690:     );
691:     path.setAttribute(
692:       'class',
693:       `arrow ${group.ready ? 'arrow--ready' : 'arrow--waiting'}`,
694:     );
695:     path.setAttribute(
696:       'marker-end',
697:       group.ready ? 'url(#arrow-gold)' : 'url(#arrow-dim)',
698:     );
699:     ui.arrows.append(path);
700: 
701:     const label = svgElement('text');
702:     label.setAttribute('x', String(0.25 * start.x + 0.5 * controlX + 0.25 * end.x));
703:     label.setAttribute('y', String(0.25 * start.y + 0.5 * controlY + 0.25 * end.y));
704:     label.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
705:     label.setAttribute('dominant-baseline', 'middle');
706:     label.setAttribute(
707:       'class',
708:       `arrow__label${group.ready ? ' arrow__label--ready' : ''}`,
709:     );
710:     label.textContent = [...group.orders].join('·');
711:     ui.arrows.append(label);
712:   }
713: }
714: 
715: /* ---------- Drag and drop ---------- */
716: 
717: function updateDragCards() {
718:   const expected = drag ? nextDestination(findPot(drag.potId))?.toId : null;
719: 
720:   for (const [id, view] of stationViews) {
721:     view.card.classList.toggle('is-valid-target', id === expected);
722:     view.card.classList.remove('is-drop-invalid');
723:   }
724: }
725: 
726: function targetUnderPointer(clientX, clientY) {
727:   const el = document.elementFromPoint(clientX, clientY);
728:   const card = el?.closest('.station');
729:   const id = card?.dataset.stationId;
730: 
731:   return id && stationViews.has(id) ? id : null;
732: }
733: 
734: function moveGhost(clientX, clientY) {
735:   if (!drag) {
736:     return;
737:   }
738:   drag.ghost.style.left = `${clientX - drag.offsetX}px`;
739:   drag.ghost.style.top = `${clientY - drag.offsetY}px`;
740: }
741: 
742: function onPointerMove(event) {
743:   if (!drag) {
744:     return;
745:   }
746: 
747:   moveGhost(event.clientX, event.clientY);
748:   const targetId = targetUnderPointer(event.clientX, event.clientY);
749: 
750:   if (targetId === drag.hoverId) {
751:     return;
752:   }
753: 
754:   drag.hoverId = targetId;
755: 
756:   for (const [id, view] of stationViews) {
757:     view.card.classList.toggle('is-drop-hover', id === targetId);
758:     view.card.classList.toggle(
759:       'is-drop-invalid',
760:       id === targetId && id !== drag.expectedId,
761:     );
762:   }
763: }
764: 
765: function cleanupDrag() {
766:   if (!drag) {
767:     return;
768:   }
769: 
770:   drag.ghost.remove();
771:   drag.originEl?.classList.remove('is-dragging-source');
772:   document.body.classList.remove('is-dragging');
773: 
774:   for (const view of stationViews.values()) {
775:     view.card.classList.remove(
776:       'is-valid-target',
777:       'is-drop-hover',
778:       'is-drop-invalid',
779:     );
780:   }
781: 
782:   window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);
783:   window.removeEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);
784:   window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerCancel);
785:   drag = null;
786: }
787: 
788: function onPointerUp(event) {
789:   if (!drag) {
790:     return;
791:   }
792: 
793:   const potId = drag.potId;
794:   const targetId = targetUnderPointer(event.clientX, event.clientY);
795: 
796:   cleanupDrag();
797: 
798:   if (targetId) {
799:     const eventResult = core.dropPot(potId, targetId);
800: 
801:     if (eventResult.type === 'rejected') {
802:       toast(rejectionText(eventResult));
803:     }
804:   }
805: 
806:   render();
807: }
808: 
809: function onPointerCancel() {
810:   cleanupDrag();
811:   render();
812: }
813: 
814: function onPointerDown(event) {
815:   if (event.button !== 0) {
816:     return;
817:   }
818: 
819:   const potEl = event.target.closest('.pot');
820:   if (!potEl || potEl.closest('.fx-layer')) {
821:     return;
822:   }
823: 
824:   const pot = findPot(Number(potEl.dataset.potId));
825:   if (!pot) {
826:     return;
827:   }
828: 
829:   if (!isDraggable(pot)) {
830:     if (
831:       pot.status === POT_STATUS.WORKING ||
832:       pot.status === POT_STATUS.BOTTLING
833:     ) {
834:       toast(`Pot #${pot.id} is still working.`);
835:     }
836:     return;
837:   }
838: 
839:   event.preventDefault();
840: 
841:   const rect = potEl.getBoundingClientRect();
842:   const ghost = potEl.cloneNode(true);
843:   ghost.classList.remove('is-working', 'is-ready', 'is-urgent', 'is-warning');
844:   ghost.classList.add('drag-ghost');
845:   ghost.style.left = `${rect.left}px`;
846:   ghost.style.top = `${rect.top}px`;
847:   ghost.style.width = `${rect.width}px`;
848:   ghost.style.height = `${rect.height}px`;
849:   ghost.tabIndex = -1;
850:   ui.fx.append(ghost);
851: 
852:   potEl.classList.add('is-dragging-source');
853: 
854:   drag = {
855:     potId: pot.id,
856:     ghost,
857:     originEl: potEl,
858:     offsetX: event.clientX - rect.left,
859:     offsetY: event.clientY - rect.top,
860:     expectedId: nextDestination(pot)?.toId ?? null,
861:     hoverId: null,
862:   };
863: 
864:   document.body.classList.add('is-dragging');
865:   updateDragCards();
866: 
867:   window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);
868:   window.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);
869:   window.addEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerCancel);
870: }
871: 
872: /* ---------- Events, FX, and hints ---------- */
873: 
874: function stationName(id) {
875:   return id === 'bottler' ? 'BOTTLER' : stationMeta.get(id)?.name ?? id;
876: }
877: 
878: function rejectionText(event) {
879:   switch (event.reason) {
880:     case 'wrong-station':
881:       return `Wrong station — that pot needs ${stationName(
882:         event.expectedStationId,
883:       )} for process ${event.process?.order}.`;
884:     case 'pot-busy':
885:       return 'That pot is still being processed.';
886:     case 'station-full':
887:       return 'That station already holds four pots.';
888:     case 'bottler-full':
889:       return 'The bottler is full. Wait for a pot to finish.';
890:     case 'processes-remaining':
891:       return `That pot still needs process ${event.process?.order} at ${stationName(
892:         event.process?.stationId,
893:       )}.`;
894:     case 'ready-for-bottler':
895:       return 'All twelve processes are done — drop it in the bottler.';
896:     case 'already-there':
897:       return 'That pot is already at this station.';
898:     case 'pot-gone':
899:       return 'That pot has already left the line.';
900:     case 'unknown-station':
901:       return 'That is not a station.';
902:     case 'unknown-pot':
903:       return 'Unknown pot.';
904:     default:
905:       return 'That move is not possible right now.';
906:   }
907: }
908: 
909: function toast(message) {
910:   ui.toast.textContent = message;
911:   ui.toast.classList.add('is-visible');
912:   window.clearTimeout(toastTimer);
913:   toastTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
914:     ui.toast.classList.remove('is-visible');
915:   }, 2100);
916: }
917: 
918: function pulseStation(id) {
919:   const view = stationViews.get(id);
920:   if (!view) {
921:     return;
922:   }
923: 
924:   view.card.classList.remove('is-pumping');
925:   void view.card.offsetWidth;
926:   view.card.classList.add('is-pumping');
927:   window.setTimeout(() => view.card.classList.remove('is-pumping'), 380);
928: }
929: 
930: function capturePotFx(potId, kind) {
931:   if (reduceMotion) {
932:     return;
933:   }
934: 
935:   const el = document.querySelector(
936:     `.pot[data-pot-id="${potId}"]:not(.drag-ghost)`,
937:   );
938:   if (!el) {
939:     return;
940:   }
941: 
942:   const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
943:   const clone = el.cloneNode(true);
944:   clone.classList.remove('is-ready', 'is-urgent', 'is-warning');
945:   clone.classList.add('fx-pot', `fx-pot--${kind}`);
946:   clone.style.left = `${rect.left}px`;
947:   clone.style.top = `${rect.top}px`;
948:   clone.style.width = `${rect.width}px`;
949:   clone.style.height = `${rect.height}px`;
950:   ui.fx.append(clone);
951: 
952:   const animation = clone.animate(
953:     kind === 'bottled'
954:       ? [
955:           { transform: 'translate(0, 0) scale(1)', opacity: 1 },
956:           {
957:             transform: `translate(0, ${window.innerHeight - rect.top + 60}px) scale(0.55)`,
958:             opacity: 0,
959:           },
960:         ]
961:       : [
962:           { transform: 'translate(0, 0) scale(1) rotate(0deg)', opacity: 1 },
963:           { transform: 'translate(0, 100px) scale(0.5) rotate(26deg)', opacity: 0 },
964:         ],
965:     {
966:       duration: 680,
967:       easing: 'ease-in',
968:       fill: 'forwards',
969:     },
970:   );
971: 
972:   animation.finished.then(() => clone.remove()).catch(() => clone.remove());
973: }
974: 
975: function spawnVials(count) {
976:   if (reduceMotion) {
977:     return;
978:   }
979: 
980:   const chute = stationViews.get('bottler')?.card.querySelector('.bottler__chute');
981:   if (!chute) {
982:     return;
983:   }
984: 
985:   const rect = chute.getBoundingClientRect();
986:   const startX = rect.right - 10;
987:   const startY = rect.top + rect.height / 2 - 13;
988:   const amount = Math.min(10, count);
989: 
990:   for (let index = 0; index < amount; index += 1) {
991:     const vial = document.createElement('img');
992:     vial.src = ASSETS.vial;
993:     vial.alt = '';
994:     vial.className = 'fx-vial';
995:     vial.style.left = `${startX}px`;
996:     vial.style.top = `${startY}px`;
997:     ui.fx.append(vial);
998: 
999:     const drift = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 70;
1000:     const dx = window.innerWidth - startX + 40;
1001:     const dy = drift;
1002: 
1003:     const animation = vial.animate(
1004:       [
1005:         { transform: 'translate(0, 0) scale(1)', opacity: 1 },
1006:         { transform: `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px) scale(0.85)`, opacity: 0 },
1007:       ],
1008:       {
1009:         duration: 620 + Math.random() * 260,
1010:         delay: index * 42,
1011:         easing: 'ease-in',
1012:         fill: 'backwards',
1013:       },
1014:     );
1015: 
1016:     animation.finished.then(() => vial.remove()).catch(() => vial.remove());
1017:   }
1018: }
1019: 
1020: function handleEvents(events) {
1021:   for (const event of events) {
1022:     switch (event.type) {
1023:       case 'process-complete':
1024:         pulseStation(event.stationId);
1025:         break;
1026:       case 'pot-spoiled':
1027:         capturePotFx(event.potId, 'spoiled');
1028:         toast(
1029:           `Pot #${event.potId} spoiled at ${stationLabel(event.stationId)} — the timer ran out.`,
1030:         );
1031:         break;
1032:       case 'pot-bottled':
1033:         capturePotFx(event.potId, 'bottled');
1034:         spawnVials(event.vials);
1035:         break;
1036:       default:
1037:         break;
1038:     }
1039:   }
1040: }
1041:

1047: });

1053: });
1054: 
1055: document.fonts?.ready.then(renderArrows).catch(() => {});
1056: 
1057: /* ---------- Boot and game loop ---------- */
1058: 
1059: ui.intake.style.setProperty('--intake-x', `${INTAKE_POSITION.x}%`);
1060: ui.intake.style.setProperty('--intake-y', `${INTAKE_POSITION.y}%`);
1061: 
1062: buildStations();
1063: buildPlan();
1064: render();
1065: 
1066: window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
1067:   renderArrows();
1068: });
1069: 
1070: function frame(now) {
1071:   const delta = Math.min(250, Math.max(0, now - lastFrame));
1072:   lastFrame = now;
1073: 
1074:   if (!document.hidden) {
1075:     const result = core.advance(delta);
1076: 
1077:     if (result.events.length > 0) {
1078:       handleEvents(result.events);
1079:       render();
1080:     } else {
1081:       updateLiveUi();
1082:     }
1083:   }
1084: 
1085:   window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
1086: }
1087: 
1088: window.requestAnimationFrame((now) => {
1089:   lastFrame = now;
1090:   window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
1091: });

20: Then open <http://localhost:8002/>.
21: 
22: ## Controls
23: 
24: | Action | Input |
25: | --- | --- |
26: | Collect an empty pot | `TAKE POT` in the top-left intake (no limit) |
27: | Move a pot | Drag it from the intake or a station and drop it on a station |
28: | Start a process | Drop a ready pot on the station for its current numbered process |
29: | Finish a pot | After process 12, drag it into the bottom-right bottler |
30: | Bank output | Vials leave the bottler automatically; `DASH` tracks seconds |
31: 
32: Stations process pots automatically. A pot turns **ready** when its process finishes, then a timer starts. Drag it to the next station before the timer empties or it spoils and is lost.
33: 
34: ## Floor
35: 
36: - **Intake** — top left, unlimited empty pots.
37: - **Six stations** — grain mill, mash vat, agitator press, spirit still, purity filter, and plan bureau. Every station has a 2×2 grid of four slots plus a machine protrusion.
38: - **Bottler** — bottom right. Four slots. Vials stream off the right edge; emptied pots fall off the bottom edge.
39: - **Info panel** — bottom left. Title, live stats, all twelve numbered processes, and the current hint.
40: - **Arrows** — drawn from each pot's station to its next required station, labelled with the process number. Bright gold arrows are ready to follow; dashed arrows are still processing.
41: 
42: ## Process plan
43: 
44: | # | Process | Station | Work | Window |
45: | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
46: | 1 | Seize the Harvest | Mill | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |
47: | 2 | Nationalize the Mash | Vat | 1400 ms | 7000 ms |
48: | 3 | Agitate the Masses | Press | 1200 ms | 6500 ms |
49: | 4 | Distill the Spirit | Still | 1900 ms | 6500 ms |
50: | 5 | Collectivize the Vat | Vat | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |
51: | 6 | Purge Deviations | Filter | 1400 ms | 6500 ms |
52: | 7 | Ratify the Plan | Bureau | 1200 ms | 7000 ms |
53: | 8 | Five-Year Ferment | Still | 2100 ms | 6500 ms |
54: | 9 | Commissar's Blend | Vat | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |
55: | 10 | Inspect the Cadre | Bureau | 1300 ms | 6500 ms |
56: | 11 | Filter the Line | Filter | 1700 ms | 6500 ms |
57: | 12 | Seal the Revolution | Press | 1400 ms | 7000 ms |
58: 
59: The vat is used three times; the still, filter, bureau, and press twice; the mill once. The bottler is the final destination.
60: 
61: ## Rules implemented
62: 
63: - The intake has no pot limit; `TAKE POT` always works.
64: - Each station holds four pots at once. A fifth drop is rejected and stays in its source.
65: - A pot can only enter the station required by its current process. A wrong station is rejected; the pot stays where it was.
66: - Stations process automatically. A working pot cannot be picked up.
67: - When work finishes the pot becomes ready and a window timer starts.
68: - If the window expires, the pot spoils, leaves the station, and frees its slot.
69: - After process 12, the pot can enter the bottler. Earlier drops are rejected.
70: - The bottler holds four pots and empties each pot over ten seconds (`bottlingMs`). The pot's liquid level visibly drains while it bottles.
71: - Each pot yields 20 vials; each vial is 1 second of dash.
72: - Vials are awarded when bottling finishes. The emptied pot leaves the floor.
73: - The core is deterministic and replayable from the same action sequence. The browser passes frame deltas to `advance()`.
74: 
75: ## Core API
76: 
77: ```js
78: import {
79:   DashSupplementFactory,
80:   POT_STATUS,
81:   PROCESSES,
82:   STATIONS,
83: } from './src/dashSupplementFactoryCore.js';
84: 
85: const factory = new DashSupplementFactory();
86: 
87: factory.takePot();                    // unlimited intake pot
88: factory.dropPot(1, 'mill');           // starts process 1
89: factory.advance(1500);                // tick: returns process-complete
90: factory.dropPot(1, 'vat');            // starts process 2
91: factory.advance(1400);
92: // ...continue through the plan...
93: factory.dropPot(1, 'bottler');        // only after process 12
94: factory.advance(10000);               // tick: returns pot-bottled
95: factory.takeVials();                  // 20, then resets
96: factory.takeSeconds();                // 20, then resets
97: ```
98: 
99: Useful getters: `processes`, `stations`, `pots`, `activePots`, `intakePots`, `occupancy`, `vialsProduced`, `secondsProduced`, `potsBottled`, `potsSpoiled`, `elapsedMs`, `stationCapacity`, and `bottlerCapacity`.
100: 
101: `dropPot()` returns `pot-dropped` or `rejected` with a reason such as `wrong-station`, `station-full`, `bottler-full`, `pot-busy`, `processes-remaining`, or `pot-gone`.
102: 
103: `advance(deltaMs)` returns `{ type: 'tick', events, elapsedMs, ... }`. Events can include `process-complete`, `pot-spoiled`, and `pot-bottled` in the same tick.
104: 
105: ## Configuration
106: 
107: | Option | Default | Meaning |
108: | --- | --- | --- |
109: | `stations` | `STATIONS` | Six station definitions |

158: Rules as implemented:
159: 
160: - The intake is unlimited.
161: - Each station holds four pots in a 2×2 grid. A fifth drop is rejected and stays at its source.
162: - A pot can only enter the station for its current process. Wrong-station drops are rejected without losing the pot.
163: - Stations process automatically; a working pot cannot leave.
164: - A finished process opens a ready window. If the window empties, the pot spoils, leaves the station, and frees its slot.
165: - Process 12 must finish before the bottler accepts the pot.
166: - The bottler holds four pots and empties each pot over `10000 ms`; the pot's liquid drains visibly while it bottles.
167: - One pot produces 20 vials; one vial is one second of dash.
168: - Vials are only awarded when bottling finishes.
169: - The core is deterministic and replayable; the browser owns the clock and reports deltas.
170: 
171: Core interface:
172: 
173: - Mutations: `takePot()`, `dropPot(potId, targetId)`, `advance(deltaMs)`.
174: - Output: `takeVials()`, `takeSeconds()`, `takeBottles()` alias.
175: - State: `processes`, `stations`, `pots`, `activePots`, `intakePots`, `occupancy`, `vialsProduced`, `secondsProduced`, `potsBottled`, `potsSpoiled`, `elapsedMs`, `stationCapacity`, `bottlerCapacity`.
176: - `dropPot()` returns `pot-dropped` or `rejected` (`wrong-station`, `station-full`, `bottler-full`, `pot-busy`, `processes-remaining`, `pot-gone`).
177: - `advance()` returns a tick with ordered `events` (`process-complete`, `pot-spoiled`, `pot-bottled`).
178: 
179: Browser shell as built:
180: 
181: - Separate station cards, no belt. Each card has a machine protrusion and four slots.
182: - Pointer drag-and-drop from the intake or a ready station to the next station or bottler.
183: - Gold arrows show the next hop for ready pots; dashed arrows show the hop still processing.
184: - The bottom-left panel shows title, IN PLAY / VIALS / DASH / SPOILED, all twelve numbered processes, and a live hint.
185: - Pots show a process badge, fill level, work progress, and a ready-window timer.
186: - Bottling takes ten seconds per pot and drains its liquid visibly, then streams vial sprites off the right edge; emptied pots fall off the bottom edge.
187: - Wrong drops raise a toast; spoiled pots are removed and counted.
188: - The line never ends or batches; play continues as long as the player takes pots.

### FX functions

Replace `capturePotFx(potId, kind)` with two functions:

```js
function clonePotVisual(potEl) {
  const clone = potEl.cloneNode(true);
  clone.classList.remove('is-ready','is-urgent','is-warning','is-working','is-bottling','is-queued','is-draggable','is-dragging-source');
  clone.removeAttribute('data-pot-id');
  clone.tabIndex = -1;
  clone.disabled = true;
  return clone;
}

function sendPotDownEmpties(potId) {
  if (reduceMotion) return;
  const track = stationViews.get('bottler')?.emptiesTrack;
  const source = document.querySelector(`.pot[data-pot-id="${potId}"]:not(.drag-ghost)`);
  if (!track || !source) return;
  const clone = clonePotVisual(source);
  clone.classList.add('is-empty', 'belt-pot');
  const badge = clone.querySelector('.pot__badge');
  if (badge) badge.textContent = '○';
  clone.style.setProperty('--fill', '0%');
  clone.style.setProperty('--liquid', 'transparent');
  track.append(clone);

  const travel = track.clientWidth + 80;
  const duration = Math.max(700, (travel / 95) * 1000);
  const animation = clone.animate(
    [
      { transform: 'translate(-10px, -66px) rotate(-10deg)' },
      { transform: 'translate(0, 0) rotate(0deg)', offset: 0.08 },
      { transform: `translate(${travel}px, 0) rotate(0deg)` },
    ],
    { duration, easing: 'linear', fill: 'forwards' },
  );
  animation.finished.then(() => clone.remove()).catch(() => clone.remove());
}
```

But `.belt-pot` position absolute left 0, top 50% margin-top -23. Transform translate 0 is at top 50% with margin. Good. First keyframe translate(-10,-66) means above belt (drop). Then to 0. Then right.

"dont fade it" — no opacity changes.

Vials:
```js
function spawnVial() {
  const track = stationViews.get('bottler')?.vialTrack;
  if (!track) return;
  const vial = document.createElement('img');
  vial.src = ASSETS.vial;
  vial.alt = '';
  vial.className = 'belt-vial';
  track.append(vial);
  const travel = track.clientWidth + 40;
  const duration = Math.max(900, (travel / 110) * 1000);
  const animation = vial.animate(
    [
      { transform: 'translate(-16px, 0)' },
      { transform: `translate(${travel}px, 0)` },
    ],
    { duration, easing: 'linear', fill: 'forwards' },
  );
  animation.finished.then(() => vial.remove()).catch(() => vial.remove());
}
```

`vialEmitted = new Map();`

```js
function vialQuota(progressMs) {
  const perVial = core.config.bottlingMs / core.config.vialsPerPot;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(core.config.vialsPerPot, Math.floor(progressMs / perVial + 1e-6)));
}

function updateBottlingFlow() {
  const active = core.pots.find(p => p.location === 'bottler' && p.status === POT_STATUS.BOTTLING);
  if (!active) return;
  const emitted = vialQuota(active.progressMs);
  const previous = vialEmitted.get(active.id) ?? 0;
  if (emitted > previous) {
    for (let i = previous; i < emitted; i += 1) spawnVial();
    vialEmitted.set(active.id, emitted);
  }
}
```
At progress 0, floor(0/500)=0. At 500 -> 1. Good. At 10000 ->20 but active gone at completion; completion handles rest.

In handleEvents:
```js
case 'pot-bottled': {
  const already = vialEmitted.get(event.potId) ?? 0;
  const remaining = Math.max(0, event.vials - already);
  for (let i = 0; i < remaining; i += 1) spawnVial();
  vialEmitted.delete(event.potId);
  sendPotDownEmpties(event.potId);
  break;
}
```

### Belt CSS

```css
/* ---------- Bottler outfeed belts ---------- */

.outfeed {
  position: absolute;
  left: calc(100% + 2px);
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-end;
  width: 440px;
  pointer-events: none;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 10px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.35));
}
.outfeed--vials { top: 48px; height: 36px; }
.outfeed--empties { top: 100px; height: 58px; }

.outfeed__label {
  position: absolute;
  top: -11px;
  left: 8px;
  color: var(--gold);
  font-size: 6.5px;
  letter-spacing: .18em;
  opacity: .85;
}
.outfeed--empties .outfeed__label { color: #8d8470; }

.belt {
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  border: 1px solid #5b5036;
  border-left: 0;
  border-radius: 0 7px 7px 0;
  background:
    linear-gradient(180deg, #2a251b, #12100a 55%, #1d1913);
  box-shadow:
    inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.08),
    inset 0 -3px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.6);
  overflow: hidden;
}
.belt::before,
.belt::after {
  content:"";
  position:absolute;
  left:0; right:0;
  height: 4px;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #6b5c39, #2a2418);
  z-index: 2;
}
.belt::before { top: 3px; }
.belt::after { bottom: 3px; }

.belt__track {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 7px 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  background:
    repeating-linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(242,201,76,.16) 0 2px, transparent 2px 18px),
    repeating-linear-gradient(90deg, #1c1810 0 14px, #242017 14px 28px);
  animation: belt-run .9s linear infinite;
}
```

46914:  "newString": "Purpose: a station-management factory game that produces dash seconds.\n\nImplemented: `dash-supplement-factory/src/dashSupplementFactoryCore.js` (pure rules, no DOM or wall-clock), `test/dashSupplementFactoryCore.test.js` (21 tests), and a browser shell in `dash-supplement-factory/` (`index.html`, `styles.css`, `src/main.js`, `src/assets.js`, `assets/bottle.svg`, `assets/vial.svg`). Run it with `python3 -m http.server 8002` from the module folder.\n\nFlow:\n\n1. Take unlimited empty pots from the top-left intake.\n2. Drag each pot into the station for its current numbered process.\n3. The station processes automatically. Move the pot before its ready window expires.\n4. Repeat through twelve processes over six reusable stations.\n5. After process 12, drag the pot into the bottom-right bottler.\n6. Bottling produces 20 vials of one-second dash; vials leave right, emptied pots leave bottom.\n\nLayout:\n\n- Intake top left.\n- Six separate stations with 2×2 slot grids and machine protrusions: mill, vat, press, still, filter, bureau.\n- Bottler bottom right.\n- Info panel bottom left with title, live stats, the full numbered process plan, and the current hint.\n- Dynamic route arrows run from each pot's station to its next required station and are labelled with the process number.\n\nProcesses as implemented:\n\n| # | Process | Station | Work | Window |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| 1 | Seize the Harvest | Mill | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |\n| 2 | Nationalize the Mash | Vat | 1400 ms | 7000 ms |\n| 3 | Agitate the Masses | Press | 1200 ms | 6500 ms |\n| 4 | Distill the Spirit | Still | 1900 ms | 6500 ms |\n| 5 | Collectivize the Vat | Vat | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |\n| 6 | Purge Deviations | Filter | 1400 ms | 6500 ms |\n| 7 | Ratify the Plan | Bureau | 1200 ms | 7000 ms |\n| 8 | Five-Year Ferment | Still | 2100 ms | 6500 ms |\n| 9 | Commissar's Blend | Vat | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |\n| 10 | Inspect the Cadre | Bureau | 1300 ms | 6500 ms |\n| 11 | Filter the Line | Filter | 1700 ms | 6500 ms |\n| 12 | Seal the Revolution | Press | 1400 ms | 7000 ms |\n\nThe vat is used three times; the still, filter, bureau, and press twice; the mill once.\n\nRules as implemented:\n\n- The intake is unlimited.\n- Each station holds four pots in a 2×2 grid. A fifth drop is rejected and stays at its source.\n- A pot can only enter the station for its current process. Wrong-station drops are rejected without losing the pot.\n- Stations process automatically; a working pot cannot leave.\n- A finished process opens a ready window. If the window empties, the pot spoils, leaves the station, and frees its slot.\n- Process 12 must finish before the bottler accepts the pot.\n- The bottler holds four pots and bottles one pot every `900 ms`.\n- One pot produces 20 vials; one vial is one second of dash.\n- Vials are only awarded when bottling finishes.\n- The core is deterministic and replayable; the browser owns the clock and reports deltas.\n\nCore interface:\n\n- Mutations: `takePot()`, `dropPot(potId, targetId)`, `advance(deltaMs)`.\n- Output: `takeVials()`, `takeSeconds()`, `takeBottles()` alias.\n- State: `processes`, `stations`, `pots`, `activePots`, `intakePots`, `occupancy`, `vialsProduced`, `secondsProduced`, `potsBottled`, `potsSpoiled`, `elapsedMs`, `stationCapacity`, `bottlerCapacity`.\n- `dropPot()` returns `pot-dropped` or `rejected` (`wrong-station`, `station-full`, `bottler-full`, `pot-busy`, `processes-remaining`, `pot-gone`).\n- `advance()` returns a tick with ordered `events` (`process-complete`, `pot-spoiled`, `pot-bottled`).\n\nBrowser shell as built:\n\n- Separate station cards, no belt. Each card has a machine protrusion and four slots.\n- Pointer drag-and-drop from the intake or a ready station to the next station or bottler.\n- Gold arrows show the next hop for ready pots; dashed arrows show the hop still processing.\n- The bottom-left panel shows title, IN PLAY / VIALS / DASH / SPOILED, all twelve numbered processes, and a live hint.\n- Pots show a process badge, fill level, work progress, and a ready-window timer.\n- Bottling streams vial sprites off the right edge; emptied pots fall off the bottom edge.\n- Wrong drops raise a toast; spoiled pots are removed and counted.\n- The line never ends or batches; play continues as long as the player takes pots.\n\nOpen questions:\n\n- Confirm one pot should yield 20 one-second vials.\n- Tune the process durations and ready windows through playtesting.\n- Confirm wrong-station drops should be free rather than spoiling or delaying the pot.\n- Decide whether the six-station, twelve-process plan is the final route.\n- Integrate `takeVials()` / `takeSeconds()` with the host speed resource.",

'/tmp/opencode/dash-layout.png';\nconst sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));\n\nconst server = spawn('python3', ['-m', 'http.server', '8002'], {\n  cwd: '/home/rabamari/Projects/netshoot/dash-supplement-factory',\n  stdio: 'ignore',\n});\nconst chrome = spawn(\n  '/opt/google/chrome/chrome',\n  [\n    '--headless=new',\n    '--no-sandbox',\n    '--disable-gpu',\n    '--hide-scrollbars',\n    '--mute-audio',\n    `--window-size=${WIDTH},${HEIGHT}`,\n    '--remote-debugging-port=9234',\n    '--user-data-dir=/tmp/opencode/chrome-dash-layout',\n    BASE,\n  ],\n  { stdio: 'ignore' },\n);\n\nlet ws;\ntry {\n  let target;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 80 && !target; i += 1) {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(`${DEBUG}/json/list`);\n      const list = await res.json();\n      target = list.find((t) => t.type === 'page' && t.url.startsWith(BASE));\n    } catch {}\n    if (!target) await sleep(150);\n  }\n  if (!target) throw new Error('no target');\n\n  ws = new WebSocket(target.webSocketDebuggerUrl);\n  await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    ws.addEventListener('open', resolve, { once: true });\n    ws.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });\n  });\n\n  let id = 0;\n  const pending = new Map();\n  ws.addEventListener('message', (event) => {\n    const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);\n    if (msg.id && pending.has(msg.id)) {\n      const { resolve, reject } = pending.get(msg.id);\n      pending.delete(msg.id);\n      if (msg.error) reject(new Error(JSON.stringify(msg.error)));\n      else resolve(msg.result);\n    }\n  });\n  const send = (method, params = {}) =>\n    new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n      const next = ++id;\n      pending.set(next, { resolve, reject });\n      ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id: next, method, params }));\n    });\n  const evaluate = async (expression) => {\n    const result = await send('Runtime.evaluate', {\n      expression,\n      awaitPromise: true,\n      returnByValue: true,\n    });\n    if (result.exceptionDetails) throw new Error(result.exceptionDetails.text);\n    return result.result.value;\n  };\n\n  await send('Runtime.enable');\n  await send('Page.enable');\n  await sleep(600);\n\n  await evaluate('for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) document.getElementById(\"take-pot\").click()');\n  await evaluate('document.querySelector(\\'#action-panel [data-step-id=\"vodka\"]\\').click()');\n  await evaluate('document.querySelector(\\'#action-panel [data-step-id=\"beet-extract\"]\\').click()');\n  await sleep(300);\n\n  const metrics = await evaluate(`({\n    scrollWidth: document.documentElement.scrollWidth,\n    innerWidth: window.innerWidth,\n    scrollHeight: document.documentElement.scrollHeight,\n    innerHeight: window.innerHeight,\n  })`);\n  console.log(JSON.stringify(metrics));\n\n  const shot = await send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });\n  writeFileSync(OUT, Buffer.from(shot.data, 'base64'));\n  console.log(`saved ${OUT}`);\n} finally {\n  ws?.close();\n  chrome.kill('SIGKILL');\n  server.kill('SIGKILL');\n}\n",
40144:  "content": "import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';\n\nconst BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8002/';\nconst DEBUG = 'http://127.0.0.1:9234';\nconst CHROME = '/opt/google/chrome/chrome';\n\nconst errors = [];\nconst results = [];\nconst sleep = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));\n\nfunction check(name, condition, detail = '') {\n  results.push({ name, ok: Boolean(condition), detail });\n  console.log(`${condition ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${name}${detail ? ` — ${detail}` : ''}`);\n}\n\nclass Session {\n  constructor(url) {\n    this.ws = new WebSocket(url);\n    this.nextId = 1;\n    this.pending = new Map();\n    this.ready = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n      this.ws.addEventListener('open', resolve, { once: true });\n      this.ws.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });\n    });\n    this.ws.addEventListener('message', (event) => {\n      const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);\n      if (msg.id && this.pending.has(msg.id)) {\n        const { resolve, reject } = this.pending.get(msg.id);\n        this.pending.delete(msg.id);\n        if (msg.error) reject(new Error(JSON.stringify(msg.error)));\n        else resolve(msg.result);\n      }\n      if (msg.method === 'Runtime.exceptionThrown') {\n        errors.push(\n          msg.params.exceptionDetails.exception?.description ??\n            msg.params.exceptionDetails.text,\n        );\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  send(method, params = {}) {\n    const id = this.nextId++;\n    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n      this.pending.set(id, { resolve, reject });\n      this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params }));\n    });\n  }\n\n  async eval(expression) {\n    const result = await this.send('Runtime.evaluate', {\n      expression,\n      awaitPromise: true,\n      returnByValue: true,\n    });\n    if (result.exceptionDetails) {\n      throw new Error(\n        result.exceptionDetails.exception?.description ?? result.exceptionDetails.text,\n      );\n    }\n    return result.result.value;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function waitForTarget() {\n  for (let i = 0; i < 80; i += 1) {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(`${DEBUG}/json/list`);\n      const targets = await res.json();\n      const page = targets.find(\n        (target) => target.type === 'page' && target.url.startsWith(BASE),\n      );\n      if (page) return page.webSocketDebuggerUrl;\n    } catch {}\n    await sleep(150);\n  }\n  throw new Error('timed out waiting for the page target');\n}\n\nconst server = spawn('python3', ['-m', 'http.server', '8002'], {\n  cwd: '/home/rabamari/Projects/netshoot/dash-supplement-factory',\n  stdio: 'ignore',\n});\n\nconst chrome = spawn(\n  CHROME,\n  [\n    '--headless=new',\n    '--no-sandbox',\n    '--disable-gpu',\n    '--hide-scrollbars',\n    '--mute-audio',\n    '--window-size=1280,860',\n    '--remote-debugging-port=9234',\n    '--user-data-dir=/tmp/opencode/chrome-dash2',\n    BASE,\n  ],\n  { stdio: 'ignore' },\n);\n\nlet session;\n\ntry {\n  const wsUrl = await waitForTarget();\n  session = new Session(wsUrl);\n  await session.ready;\n  await session.send('Runtime.enable');\n  await session.send('Page.enable');\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i += 1) {\n    const ready = await session.eval(\n      'document.readyState === \"complete\" && Boolean(document.querySelector(\".station\"))',\n    );\n    if (ready) break;\n    await sleep(150);\n  }\n\n  check(\n    'eight stations built',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".station\").length')) === 8,\n  );\n  check(\n    'hopper starts active',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelector(\".station.is-active\").dataset.stationId')) ===\n      'hopper',\n  );\n  check(\n    'tray starts with five empty slots',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".tray__slot\").length')) === 5,\n  );\n\n  const hopperX = await session.eval(\n    'new DOMMatrix(getComputedStyle(document.getElementById(\"tray\")).transform).m41',\n  );\n\n  await session.eval(\n    'for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"hopper\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n\n  check(\n    'three pots sit on the tray',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".tray__slot .pot\").length')) === 3,\n  );\n  check(\n    'batch number starts at 001',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"batch-no\").textContent')) === '001',\n  );\n\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"vodka\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n  await sleep(750);\n\n  const vodkaActive = await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\".station.is-active\").dataset.stationId',\n  );\n  const beetX = await session.eval(\n    'new DOMMatrix(getComputedStyle(document.getElementById(\"tray\")).transform).m41',\n  );\n  check('vodka advances the line to beet extract', vodkaActive === 'beet-extract');\n  check('tray rides to the next station', beetX > hopperX + 20);\n\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"beet-extract\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n\n  await session.eval(`(async () => {\n    const button = document.querySelector('.station[data-station-id=\"stir\"] .control');\n    button.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown', { bubbles: true, button: 0 }));\n    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2200));\n    window.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerup', { bubbles: true }));\n  })()`);\n\n  check(\n    'stir advances to national spirit',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelector(\".station.is-active\").dataset.stationId')) ===\n      'national-spirit',\n  );\n\n  const shot = await session.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });\n  writeFileSync('/tmp/opencode/dash-line-processing.png', Buffer.from(shot.data, 'base64'));\n\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"national-spirit\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n\n  await session.eval(`(async () => {\n    const button = document.querySelector('.station[data-station-id=\"heat\"] .control');\n    button.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown', { bubbles: true, button: 0 }));\n    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2600));\n    window.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerup', { bubbles: true }));\n  })()`);\n\n  await session.eval(`(async () => {\n    const button = document.querySelector('.station[data-station-id=\"filter\"] .control');\n    button.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown', { bubbles: true, button: 0 }));\n    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 1800));\n    window.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerup', { bubbles: true }));\n  })()`);\n\n  await sleep(1200);\n\n  check(\n    'the belt transfers the batch to the bottler',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelector(\".station.is-active\").dataset.stationId')) ===\n      'bottler',\n  );\n  check(\n    'pots load into the machine',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".machine__pot\").length')) === 3,\n  );\n  check(\n    'tray is empty after the transfer',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".tray__slot .pot\").length')) === 0,\n  );\n  check(\n    'bottler offers the batch run',\n    (await session.eval(\n      'document.querySelector(\".station[data-station-id=\\\\\"bottler\\\\\"] .control\").textContent',\n    )) === 'BOTTLE BATCH',\n  );\n\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"bottler\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n  await sleep(2600);\n\n  check(\n    'three pots bank sixty seconds',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"dash-stat\").textContent')) === '60s',\n  );\n  check(\n    'completion card shows the batch',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"outcome-title\").textContent')) ===\n      'BATCH 001 COMPLETE',\n  );\n  check(\n    'crate is full',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelector(\".crate__text\").textContent')) ===\n      '60 / 60 BOTTLES',\n  );\n\n  const completeShot = await session.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });\n  writeFileSync('/tmp/opencode/dash-line-complete.png', Buffer.from(completeShot.data, 'base64'));\n\n  await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"outcome-action\").click()');\n  await sleep(800);\n\n  check(\n    'new batch returns to the hopper',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelector(\".station.is-active\").dataset.stationId')) ===\n      'hopper',\n  );\n  check(\n    'tray resets empty',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".tray__slot .pot\").length')) === 0,\n  );\n\n  // Wrong ingredient.\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"hopper\"] .control\\').click(); document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"hopper\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"vodka\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"national-spirit\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n  await sleep(200);\n\n  check(\n    'wrong ingredient wastes the batch',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"outcome-title\").textContent')) ===\n      'BATCH 002 WASTED',\n  );\n  check(\n    'wasted pots stay on the tray',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".tray__slot .pot.is-wasted\").length')) === 2,\n  );\n\n  await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"outcome-action\").click()');\n  await sleep(700);\n\n  // Early release.\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"hopper\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"vodka\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n  await session.eval(\n    'document.querySelector(\\'.station[data-station-id=\"beet-extract\"] .control\\').click()',\n  );\n  await session.eval(`(async () => {\n    const button = document.querySelector('.station[data-station-id=\"stir\"] .control');\n    button.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown', { bubbles: true, button: 0 }));\n    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 450));\n    window.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerup', { bubbles: true }));\n  })()`);\n  await sleep(150);\n\n  check(\n    'early release wastes the batch',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"outcome-title\").textContent')) ===\n      'BATCH 003 WASTED',\n  );\n  check(\n    'early release reason is shown',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"outcome-detail\").textContent')).includes(\n      'released too soon',\n    ),\n  );\n\n  const wastedShot = await session.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });\n  writeFileSync('/tmp/opencode/dash-line-wasted.png', Buffer.from(wastedShot.data, 'base64'));\n} catch (error) {\n  console.error('SMOKE ERROR', error);\n  results.push({ name: 'suite', ok: false, detail: String(error) });\n} finally {\n  session?.ws.close();\n  chrome.kill('SIGKILL');\n  server.kill('SIGKILL');\n}\n\nif (errors.length > 0) {\n  console.log('\\nPage errors:');\n  for (const error of errors) console.log(error);\n}\n\nconst failed = results.filter((result) => !result.ok);\nconsole.log(`\\n${results.length - failed.length}/${results.length} checks passed`);\nprocess.exit(failed.length === 0 && errors.length === 0 ? 0 : 1);\n",

'PASS' : 'FAIL'}  ${name}${detail ? ` — ${detail}` : ''}`);\n}\n\nclass Session {\n  constructor(url) {\n    this.ws = new WebSocket(url);\n    this.nextId = 1;\n    this.pending = new Map();\n    this.ready = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n      this.ws.addEventListener('open', resolve, { once: true });\n      this.ws.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });\n    });\n    this.ws.addEventListener('message', (event) => {\n      const msg = JSON.parse(event.data);\n      if (msg.id && this.pending.has(msg.id)) {\n        const { resolve, reject } = this.pending.get(msg.id);\n        this.pending.delete(msg.id);\n        if (msg.error) reject(new Error(JSON.stringify(msg.error)));\n        else resolve(msg.result);\n      }\n      if (msg.method === 'Runtime.exceptionThrown') {\n        errors.push(\n          msg.params.exceptionDetails.exception?.description ??\n            msg.params.exceptionDetails.text,\n        );\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  send(method, params = {}) {\n    const id = this.nextId++;\n    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n      this.pending.set(id, { resolve, reject });\n      this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params }));\n    });\n  }\n\n  async eval(expression) {\n    const result = await this.send('Runtime.evaluate', {\n      expression,\n      awaitPromise: true,\n      returnByValue: true,\n    });\n    if (result.exceptionDetails) {\n      throw new Error(\n        result.exceptionDetails.exception?.description ?? result.exceptionDetails.text,\n      );\n    }\n    return result.result.value;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function waitForTarget() {\n  for (let i = 0; i < 80; i += 1) {\n    try {\n      const res = await fetch(`${DEBUG}/json/list`);\n      const targets = await res.json();\n      const page = targets.find(\n        (target) => target.type === 'page' && target.url.startsWith(BASE),\n      );\n      if (page) return page.webSocketDebuggerUrl;\n    } catch {}\n    await sleep(150);\n  }\n  throw new Error('timed out waiting for the page target');\n}\n\nconst server = spawn('python3', ['-m', 'http.server', '8003'], {\n  cwd: '/home/rabamari/Projects/netshoot/dash-supplement-factory',\n  stdio: 'ignore',\n});\n\nconst chrome = spawn(\n  CHROME,\n  [\n    '--headless=new',\n    '--no-sandbox',\n    '--disable-gpu',\n    '--hide-scrollbars',\n    '--mute-audio',\n    '--window-size=1280,860',\n    '--remote-debugging-port=9235',\n    '--user-data-dir=/tmp/opencode/chrome-dash-mgmt',\n    BASE,\n  ],\n  { stdio: 'ignore' },\n);\n\nlet session;\n\nasync function dragPot(potId, stationId) {\n  return session.eval(`(() => {\n    const pot = document.querySelector('.pot[data-pot-id=\"${potId}\"]');\n    if (!pot) return { ok: false, reason: 'no-pot' };\n    const card = document.querySelector('.station[data-station-id=\"${stationId}\"]');\n    if (!card) return { ok: false, reason: 'no-card' };\n    const p = pot.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const t = card.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const at = (x, y) => ({ bubbles: true, cancelable: true, button: 0, pointerId: 1, pointerType: 'mouse', clientX: x, clientY: y });\n    pot.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown', at(p.left + p.width / 2, p.top + p.height / 2)));\n    window.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointermove', at(t.left + t.width / 2, t.top + t.height / 2)));\n    window.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerup', at(t.left + t.width / 2, t.top + t.height / 2)));\n    return { ok: true };\n  })()`);\n}\n\ntry {\n  const wsUrl = await waitForTarget();\n  session = new Session(wsUrl);\n  await session.ready;\n  await session.send('Runtime.enable');\n  await session.send('Page.enable');\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i += 1) {\n    const ready = await session.eval(\n      'document.readyState === \"complete\" && Boolean(document.querySelector(\".station\"))',\n    );\n    if (ready) break;\n    await sleep(150);\n  }\n\n  check(\n    'seven station cards are on the floor',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".station\").length')) === 7,\n  );\n  check(\n    'twelve numbered processes are in the plan',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".plan-step\").length')) === 12,\n  );\n  check(\n    'info panel is bottom-left',\n    await session.eval(`(() => {\n      const r = document.getElementById('infopanel').getBoundingClientRect();\n      return r.left < 40 && window.innerHeight - r.bottom < 40 && r.width > 300;\n    })()`),\n  );\n  check(\n    'intake is top-left',\n    await session.eval(`(() => {\n      const r = document.getElementById('intake').getBoundingClientRect();\n      return r.left < 140 && r.top < 180;\n    })()`),\n  );\n  check(\n    'bottler is bottom-right',\n    await session.eval(`(() => {\n      const r = document.querySelector('.station[data-station-id=\"bottler\"]').getBoundingClientRect();\n      return window.innerWidth - r.right < 280 && window.innerHeight - r.bottom < 200;\n    })()`),\n  );\n\n  const initialShot = await session.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });\n  writeFileSync('/tmp/opencode/dash-mgmt-initial.png', Buffer.from(initialShot.data, 'base64'));\n\n  await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"take-pot\").click(); document.getElementById(\"take-pot\").click(); document.getElementById(\"take-pot\").click()');\n\n  check(\n    'three empty pots wait in the intake',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\"#intake-rack .pot\").length')) === 3,\n  );\n\n  await dragPot(1, 'mill');\n  await sleep(300);\n\n  check(\n    'pot starts at the grain mill',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".station[data-station-id=\\\\\"mill\\\\\"] .slot .pot\").length')) === 1,\n  );\n  check(\n    'the first process is live in the plan',\n    await session.eval('document.querySelector(\".plan-step.is-live .plan-step__num\")?.textContent === \"01\"'),\n  );\n\n  await sleep(1500);\n\n  check(\n    'the mill pot becomes ready',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelector(\".station[data-station-id=\\\\\"mill\\\\\"] .pot\")?.classList.contains(\"is-ready\")')) === true,\n  );\n  check(\n    'a gold route arrow points onward',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".arrow--ready\").length')) >= 1,\n  );\n\n  const midShot = await session.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });\n  writeFileSync('/tmp/opencode/dash-mgmt-ready.png', Buffer.from(midShot.data, 'base64'));\n\n  await dragPot(1, 'still');\n  await sleep(150);\n\n  check(\n    'wrong station is rejected',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"toast\").textContent')).includes('Wrong station'),\n  );\n  check(\n    'the pot stays at the mill after rejection',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".station[data-station-id=\\\\\"mill\\\\\"] .pot\").length')) === 1,\n  );\n\n  await dragPot(1, 'vat');\n  await sleep(300);\n\n  check(\n    'the ready pot moves to the mash vat',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".station[data-station-id=\\\\\"vat\\\\\"] .pot\").length')) === 1,\n  );\n\n  await sleep(1400);\n\n  // Walk the rest of the route.\n  const route = [\n    ['press', 1300],\n    ['still', 2000],\n    ['vat', 1600],\n    ['filter', 1500],\n    ['bureau', 1300],\n    ['still', 2200],\n    ['vat', 1600],\n    ['bureau', 1400],\n    ['filter', 1800],\n    ['press', 1500],\n  ];\n\n  for (const [station, wait] of route) {\n    await dragPot(1, station);\n    await sleep(wait);\n  }\n\n  const lastShot = await session.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 'png' });\n  writeFileSync('/tmp/opencode/dash-mgmt-route.png', Buffer.from(lastShot.data, 'base64'));\n\n  await dragPot(1, 'bottler');\n  await sleep(1200);\n\n  check(\n    'bottling banks twenty vials',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"vials-stat\").textContent')) === '20',\n  );\n  check(\n    'bottling banks twenty seconds of dash',\n    (await session.eval('document.getElementById(\"dash-stat\").textContent')) === '20s',\n  );\n  check(\n    'the bottler empties its slot',\n    (await session.eval('document.querySelectorAll(\".station[data-station-id=\\\\\"bottler\\\\\"] .pot\").length')) === 0,\n  );\n\n  const bottledShot = await session.send('Page.captureScreenshot', { format: 2: 
3: - Multiplayer maze shooter (browser-based)
4: - Large scale: 1000s of concurrent players across 200 cities globally
5: - Goal: be the last team standing
6: -the entire game is minimal in design and assets with the theme of communism(cause commieD=comm(unist)i(com)eD(y))
7: - Teams are divided uniformly.
8: - Teams share everything(from ammo, dashes, and even HP)
9: - If the team HP goes to 0, the entire team dies.
10: - on a team dying, only the n/2 best players will be revived with team hp also getting halved(number of plrs\*hp per players= team HP, simpool)
11: - dead players(the n/2 not selected to play), act as ghosts, who search and collect resources for the team.
12: - on the number of players in team reaching one, the team dies. and is eliminated.
13: 04 — ghost mode

15:      a clicker game where you need to "CONTRIBUTE" your soul to your comrades(communist game intensifies). To create

36: - Normal regeneration does not happen instantly.
37: - Teams with ghosts cannot regenerate HP normally.
38: - Teams with ghosts depend on ghost-generated HP instead.
