1: # commieD — Minigame Specs
2: 
3: Status: Soul Clicker, Bullet Press, and Dash Supplement Factory are implemented under their own folders; Stewed Luck is not started. `idea.md` is still the product source of truth; this file records the working details and open questions for the ghost minigames.
4: 
5: ## Shared contract
6: 
7: - Minigames are self-contained modules: explicit state/input in, resources/events out.
8: - Do not own the main game loop, networking, or global game state.
9: - Rules must be deterministic and testable; avoid wall-clock time.
10: - Use dummy/placeholder assets everywhere for now; keep asset paths centralized.
11: 
12: ## 1. Soul Clicker (Soul Contribution)
13: 
14: Purpose: a ghost contributes their soul to increase the team's HP.
15: 
16: Implemented: `soul-clicker/` (ES modules, no dependencies). Tests run with `node --test`.
17: 
18: Rules as implemented:
19: 
20: - Click the central soul sprite to contribute.
21: - CPS is measured over a rolling 10-second window.
22: - At `CPS >= 5`: add HP at `0.2 × CPS` per second. At exactly 5 CPS this is the specified `+1 HP/s`.
23: - At `CPS < 5`: lose `0.2 HP/s` continuously.
24: - HP is settled immediately on every click (and every frame), so crossing 5 CPS switches from loss to gain at the click itself.
25: - Team HP has **no maximum cap**.
26: - HP bar spans half the screen at the top and shows the numeric HP.
27: - With `baseline = 100 × living teammates`, the bar is:
28:   - **red** when `hp < baseline`,
29:   - **yellow** when `baseline <= hp <= baseline + 90`,
30:   - **green** when `hp > baseline + 90`.
31: - The bar fill is scaled to `baseline + 90`, clamped at 100%; the numeric HP keeps growing beyond it.
32: - Random shapes appear every 10–30 seconds at random screen positions. Clicking one grants `+5 HP`; missing it has no penalty.
33: - Shape types: circle, square, triangle, diamond, star.
34: - Press **Q** to pause/resume. Pausing freezes HP gain/loss, the CPS window, and shape timers; pause duration is removed from game time.
35: - Visual effects and polish are deferred; the UI is intentionally minimal and uses placeholder assets (flat shapes, no fades, glows, or transitions).
36: 
37: Confirmed by user:
38: 
39: - Central clickable soul creates team HP.
40: - Revived teams with ghosts have no HP cap because they cannot regenerate.
41: - Red / yellow / green band thresholds above.
42: 
43: Assumed (change if wrong):
44: 
45: - HP gain keeps scaling above 5 CPS (`0.2 × CPS`) rather than capping at `1 HP/s`.
46: - Each collected shape gives `+5 HP`; missed shapes have no penalty.
47: - The minimum HP floor is `0`.
48: - Ghost stacking behaviour is deferred to the host game; the module tracks one ghost's contribution.
49: 
50: Open questions:
51: 
52: - Define the bank cap/decay once the host team economy exists.
53: - Define how multiple ghosts on one team stack.
54: - Confirm the `+5 HP` shape bonus and whether missing should hurt.
55: 
56: ## 2. Bullet Press
57: 
58: Purpose: ghosts produce magazines that living teammates load into their guns.
59: 
60: Implemented: `bullet-press/` (ES modules, no dependencies). Tests run with `node --test`.
61: 
62: Layout (top to bottom): sequence presenter → centered full-width magazine line → branch lines → single horizontal feeder. The current magazine sits at the center of the screen, with two empty magazines queued to its left and the previous magazine to its right. The feeder enters from the right and stops at the leftmost branch, so it serves only this station. It sits above the 10 branch layers. Ten vertical branch lines rise from it, one per slot, each carrying a column of bullets up to the magazine. The feeder is clogged while the branches are full: loading a slot pulls the top bullet from its branch into the magazine, refills that branch from the feeder, and indexes the feeder forward by exactly one bullet. Bullets and feed lines are centered on their slots. Magazine and sequence objects use a 5% larger scale.
63: 
64: Rules as implemented:
65: 
66: - One sequence fills one magazine. The sequence is 10 characters drawn from `A–Z`.
67: - The player types the sequence in order; lowercase input is accepted.
68: - A correct key pulls the top bullet from that slot's branch into the magazine, refills the branch from the feeder, indexes the feeder by one bullet, and fills the slot green.
69: - A wrong key pulls the top bullet in red, refills the branch, indexes the feeder, fills the slot red, and marks the whole magazine broken.
70: - A broken magazine **cannot be discarded immediately**. It stays until every slot is filled.
71: - When all slots are filled, the line shifts right: the current magazine becomes the previous one, a waiting magazine moves to the center, and a new empty magazine enters from the left. Older previous magazines slide farther right and fade out.
72: - A perfect magazine produces `multiplier` magazines.
73: - A broken magazine produces nothing and increments the broken count.
74: - The multiplier is a **whole number from 1 to 5**, based on average time per key across the completed magazine:
75:   - `≤ 400 ms/key` → `x5`
76:   - `≤ 600 ms/key` → `x4`
77:   - `≤ 800 ms/key` → `x3`
78:   - `≤ 1100 ms/key` → `x2`
79:   - slower → `x1`
80: - The timer starts when the magazine docks, not while it slides in.
81: - Production is an integer; `takeMagazines()` returns whole magazines to the host.
82: - Sequences come from a seeded RNG (or an injected RNG), never `Math.random()`.
83: 
84: Assumed (change if wrong):
85: 
86: - The sequence length and the number of magazine slots are the same value.
87: - Letters only (no digits or punctuation), to avoid confusion such as `0` vs `O`.
88: - Input is keyboard-only; no on-screen keypad yet.
89: - Speed is the only multiplier input; accuracy only decides perfect vs broken.
90: - A broken magazine awards `x0` because it produces nothing.
91: - The speed thresholds are placeholders for playtesting.
92: - No Q pause; `Q` is a valid sequence character.
93: 
94: Open questions:
95: 
96: - Tune the speed thresholds to the real feel of the game.
97: - Confirm that a broken magazine should award `x0` rather than a low whole multiplier.
98: - Confirm whether production should stay whole magazines in the host economy.
99: 
100: ## 3. Stewed Luck
101: 
102: Purpose: spend killmit earned by live-player kills to spin the communist stew pot for a special item/power-up.
103: 
104: Rules:
105: 
106: - Each live-player kill grants killmit to the team.
107: - Spend killmit to spin the pot.
108: - The spin returns a random special power-up.
109: - The pot is a "stewed luck" / communist pot edition wheel.
110: 
111: Open questions:
112: 
113: - Define the power-up list and spin weights.
114: - Define spin cost, duplicate handling, duration, and whether one player can spam spins.
115: 
116: ## 4. Dash Supplement Factory
117: 
118: Purpose: a station-management factory game that produces dash seconds.
119: 
120: Implemented: `dash-supplement-factory/src/dashSupplementFactoryCore.js` (pure rules, no DOM or wall-clock), `test/dashSupplementFactoryCore.test.js` (21 tests), and a browser shell in `dash-supplement-factory/` (`index.html`, `styles.css`, `src/main.js`, `src/assets.js`, `assets/bottle.svg`, `assets/vial.svg`). Run it with `python3 -m http.server 8002` from the module folder.
121: 
122: Flow:
123: 
124: 1. Take unlimited empty pots from the top-left intake.
125: 2. Drag each pot into the station for its current numbered process.
126: 3. The station processes automatically. Move the pot before its ready window expires.
127: 4. Repeat through twelve processes over six reusable stations.
128: 5. After process 12, drag the pot into the bottom-right bottler.
129: 6. Bottling produces 20 vials of one-second dash; vials leave right, emptied pots leave bottom.
130: 
131: Layout:
132: 
133: - Intake top left.
134: - Six separate stations with 2×2 slot grids and machine protrusions: mill, vat, press, still, filter, bureau.
135: - Bottler bottom right.
136: - Info panel bottom left with title, live stats, the full numbered process plan, and the current hint.
137: - Dynamic route arrows run from each pot's station to its next required station and are labelled with the process number.
138: 
139: Processes as implemented:
140: 
141: | # | Process | Station | Work | Window |
142: | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
143: | 1 | Seize the Harvest | Mill | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |
144: | 2 | Nationalize the Mash | Vat | 1400 ms | 7000 ms |
145: | 3 | Agitate the Masses | Press | 1200 ms | 6500 ms |
146: | 4 | Distill the Spirit | Still | 1900 ms | 6500 ms |
147: | 5 | Collectivize the Vat | Vat | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |
148: | 6 | Purge Deviations | Filter | 1400 ms | 6500 ms |
149: | 7 | Ratify the Plan | Bureau | 1200 ms | 7000 ms |
150: | 8 | Five-Year Ferment | Still | 2100 ms | 6500 ms |
151: | 9 | Commissar's Blend | Vat | 1500 ms | 7000 ms |
152: | 10 | Inspect the Cadre | Bureau | 1300 ms | 6500 ms |
153: | 11 | Filter the Line | Filter | 1700 ms | 6500 ms |
154: | 12 | Seal the Revolution | Press | 1400 ms | 7000 ms |
155: 
156: The vat is used three times; the still, filter, bureau, and press twice; the mill once.
157: 
158: Rules as implemented:
159: 
160: - The intake is unlimited.
161: - Each station holds four pots in a 2×2 grid. A fifth drop is rejected and stays at its source.
162: - A pot can only enter the station for its current process. Wrong-station drops are rejected without losing the pot.
163: - Stations process automatically; a working pot cannot leave.
164: - A finished process opens a ready window. If the window empties, the pot spoils, leaves the station, and frees its slot.
165: - Process 12 must finish before the bottler accepts the pot.
166: - The bottler holds four pots and empties them one at a time over `10000 ms` each; waiting pots queue, and only the active pot's liquid drains visibly.
167: - One pot produces 20 vials; one vial is one second of dash.
168: - Vials are only awarded when bottling finishes. Vials appear one at a time during the drain and ride the upper outfeed belt; the emptied pot rides the lower belt away without fading.
169: - The core is deterministic and replayable; the browser owns the clock and reports deltas.
170: 
171: Core interface:
172: 
173: - Mutations: `takePot()`, `dropPot(potId, targetId)`, `advance(deltaMs)`.
174: - Output: `takeVials()`, `takeSeconds()`, `takeBottles()` alias.
175: - State: `processes`, `stations`, `pots`, `activePots`, `intakePots`, `occupancy`, `vialsProduced`, `secondsProduced`, `potsBottled`, `potsSpoiled`, `elapsedMs`, `stationCapacity`, `bottlerCapacity`.
176: - `dropPot()` returns `pot-dropped` or `rejected` (`wrong-station`, `station-full`, `bottler-full`, `pot-busy`, `processes-remaining`, `pot-gone`).
177: - `advance()` returns a tick with ordered `events` (`process-complete`, `pot-spoiled`, `pot-bottled`).
178: 
179: Browser shell as built:
180: 
181: - Separate station cards with no belt between stations. Each card has a machine protrusion and four slots. The bottler has two short outfeed belts: vials above, empties below.
182: - Pointer drag-and-drop from the intake or a ready station to the next station or bottler.
183: - Gold arrows show the next hop for ready pots; dashed arrows show the hop still processing.
184: - The bottom-left panel shows title, IN PLAY / VIALS / DASH / SPOILED, all twelve numbered processes, and a live hint.

1: # Soul Clicker (Soul Contribution)
2: 
3: A ghost minigame for **commieD**. Click your soul to contribute HP to the living teammates.
4: 
5: This module is intentionally small and self-contained:
6: 
7: - `src/soulClickerCore.js` — pure rules and state (CPS window, HP gain/loss, color bands). No DOM, no timers.
8: - `src/clock.js` — pause-aware game clock; freezes time without shifting timestamps.
9: - `src/shapes.js` — random shape plans (type, position, size, delay, lifetime).
10: - `src/main.js` — browser wiring: HUD, soul clicks, shape spawning.
11: - `assets/soul-placeholder.svg` — dummy central soul sprite. Replace with real art later.
12: 
13: All assets are placeholders and paths are kept in one place (`index.html` + `main.js`). Visual polish is intentionally deferred until real art arrives.
14: 
15: ## Rules implemented
16: 
17: - CPS is measured over a rolling 10-second window.
18: - At `CPS >= 5`, HP changes at `0.2 × CPS` HP/s (so 5 CPS = `+1 HP/s`).
19: - Below 5 CPS, HP drops by `0.2 HP/s`.
20: - Team HP has **no maximum cap**.
21: - HP bar bands, with `baseline = 100 × living teammates`:
22:   - **red** below baseline,
23:   - **yellow** from baseline through `baseline + 90`,
24:   - **green** above `baseline + 90`.
25: - Random shapes appear every 10–30 seconds at random screen positions. Clicking one grants `+5 HP`. Missing it has no penalty.
26: - The HP bar fill is scaled to `baseline + 90` and clamps at 100%; the numeric HP keeps growing past that.
27: 
28: ## Run
29: 
30: ES modules need an HTTP server (opening `index.html` directly will not load the module).
31: 
32: ```bash
33: cd soul-clicker
34: python3 -m http.server 8000
35: ```
36: 
37: Then open <http://localhost:8000/>.
38: 
39: ## Controls
40: 
41: - **Click the soul** to contribute.
42: - **Q** toggles pause. Pausing freezes HP gain/loss, the CPS window, and shape timers; resuming continues from the same game time.
43: 
44: ### URL parameters
45: 
46: | Param        | Default | Meaning                                      |
47: | ------------ | ------- | -------------------------------------------- |
48: | `teammates`  | `1`     | Living teammates; sets the HP baseline        |
49: | `hp`         | baseline| Starting team HP                              |
50: | `shapeMinMs` | `10000` | Minimum delay between shapes (ms)             |
51: | `shapeMaxMs` | `30000` | Maximum delay between shapes (ms)             |
52: 
53: Example: `http://localhost:8000/?teammates=4&hp=450&shapeMinMs=3000&shapeMaxMs=6000`
54: 
55: ## Test
56: 
57: Uses Node's built-in test runner; no dependencies.
58: 
59: ```bash
60: cd soul-clicker
61: npm test
62: ```
63: 
64: or directly:
65: 
66: ```bash
67: node --test
68: ```
69: 
70: ## Integration notes
71: 
72: - The core exposes `SoulClicker` with `click(nowMs)`, `advanceTo(nowMs)`, `tick(nowMs, deltaSeconds)`, `cpsAt(nowMs)`, `band`, `hp`, `baseline`, `fillRatio`, `addShapeBonus()`, and `setLivingTeammates(count)`.
73: - `click(nowMs)` settles the pending HP change immediately, then records the click. `advanceTo(nowMs)` does the same on every frame, so HP add/subtract follows the CPS counter without waiting.
74: - `nowMs` is host-supplied; rules never read the wall clock. The browser layer owns `performance.now()` and `requestAnimationFrame`.
75: - The host game should feed the team's living-player count and consume the resulting HP value/resources.

1: # Bullet Press
2: 
3: A ghost minigame for **commieD**. One sequence fills one magazine. Type the displayed characters to pull bullets up into the magazine; wrong keys leave red slots. When every slot is filled, the magazine rolls out to the right and the next one enters from the left.
4: 
5: This module is self-contained:
6: 
7: - `src/bulletPressCore.js` — pure rules: sequences, slots, speed multiplier, production. No DOM or timers.
8: - `src/main.js` — browser wiring: keyboard input, assembly-line animation, bullet feeds, elapsed timer.
9: - `index.html` + `styles.css` — minimal placeholder UI. No image assets yet.
10: 
11: ## Layout
12: 
13: From top to bottom:
14: 
15: 1. **Sequence presenter** — the characters to type; the next one is highlighted. It sits above the magazine line.
16: 2. **Magazine line** — a full-width conveyor centered in the middle of the screen. The current 10-slot magazine sits on the line, with two empty magazines queued to its left and the previous magazine (if any) to its right.
17: 3. **Main feeder + branches** — a single horizontal bullet rail enters from the right and stops at the leftmost branch, so it only serves this station. Ten vertical feed lines branch upward from it, one per slot, each carrying a column of bullets. The rail sits above the branch layers and stays clogged while every branch is full. Loading a slot pulls its top bullet into the magazine and feeds a fresh bullet from the rail into the bottom of that branch, which indexes the rail forward by one bullet. A wrong key does the same and fills the line red.
18: 4. When all slots are filled, the line shifts right: the current magazine becomes the previous one, a waiting magazine slides into the center, and a fresh empty magazine enters from the left. Older previous magazines slide farther right and fade out.
19: 5. Magazine and sequence objects use a 5% larger scale than the base layout.
20: 
21: ## Rules implemented
22: 
23: - One sequence is 10 characters drawn from `A–Z`.
24: - A correct key fills the next magazine slot green, pulls that branch’s top bullet into the magazine, refills the bottom of that branch from the main feeder, indexes the feeder forward by one bullet, and fills the line upward.
25: - A wrong key fills the next slot red, pulls the top bullet in red, refills that branch from the feeder, indexes the feeder, and marks the whole magazine broken.
26: - A broken magazine **cannot be discarded immediately**. It stays until every slot is filled.
27: - Once all slots are filled, the magazine shifts right, becomes the previous magazine, and a waiting magazine moves into the center.
28: - A perfect magazine produces `multiplier` magazines.
29: - A broken magazine produces nothing and increments the broken count.
30: - The multiplier is a **whole number from 1 to 5**, based on typing speed across the completed magazine:
31: 
32: | Average time per key | Multiplier |
33: | -------------------- | ---------- |
34: | ≤ 400 ms             | x5         |
35: | ≤ 600 ms             | x4         |
36: | ≤ 800 ms             | x3         |
37: | ≤ 1100 ms            | x2         |
38: | slower               | x1         |
39: 
40: - A broken magazine awards `x0` because it produces nothing.
41: - Production is an integer; `takeMagazines()` returns the whole magazines produced and resets the counter.
42: - The timer starts when a magazine reaches the center (`start(nowMs)`), not while it is sliding in.
43: - Sequences come from a seeded RNG (or an injected RNG), never `Math.random()`.
44: - There is no Q pause; `Q` is a valid sequence character.
45: 
46: ## Controls
47: 
48: - Type the highlighted character with the keyboard.
49: - Letter keys only; digits, punctuation, and modifiers are ignored.
50: 
51: ## Run
52: 
53: ES modules need an HTTP server:
54: 
55: ```bash
56: cd bullet-press
57: python3 -m http.server 8001
58: ```
59: 
60: Open <http://localhost:8001/>.
61: 
62: ## Test
63: 
64: Uses Node's built-in test runner; no dependencies.
65: 
66: ```bash
67: cd bullet-press
68: npm test
69: ```
70: 
71: or directly:
72: 
73: ```bash
74: node --test
75: ```
76: 
77: ## Integration notes
78: 
79: - `BulletPress` exposes `sequence`, `slots`, `index`, `filled`, `complete`, `brokenMagazine`, `multiplierForDuration(durationMs)`, `lastMultiplier`, `lastDurationMs`, `production`, `magazinesProduced`, and `brokenMagazines`.
80: - `start(nowMs)` begins the current magazine’s timer once it is docked.
81: - `press(char, nowMs)` returns an event with `type` (`correct` / `wrong` / `ignored` / `not-ready`), `slotIndex`, `complete`, `broken`, `multiplier`, `durationMs`, and the current production.
82: - `nextMagazine(nowMs)` starts a fresh sequence only after the current magazine is complete. Omit `nowMs` and call `start(nowMs)` when the magazine docks.
83: - `takeMagazines()` is the resource-out call for the host game.
84: - Sequences are deterministic from a seed or an injected RNG.
85: 
86: ## Assumptions
87: 
88: - Letters only (no digits or punctuation), to avoid confusion such as `0` vs `O`.
89: - The sequence length and magazine slots are the same value (`sequenceLength`).
90: - Speed is the only multiplier input; accuracy only decides whether the magazine is perfect or broken.
91: - The speed thresholds are placeholders and should be tuned with playtesting.
92: - A broken magazine is counted once, when it finishes and is ejected.

+# commieD-Idea
+
+- Multiplayer maze shooter (browser-based)
+- Large scale: 1000s of concurrent players across 200 cities globally
+- Goal: be the last team standing
+-the entire game is minimal in design and assets with the theme of communism(cause commieD=comm(unist)i(com)eD(y))
+- Teams are divided uniformly.
+- Teams share everything(from ammo, dashes, and even HP)
+- If the team HP goes to 0, the entire team dies.
+- on a team dying, only the n/2 best players will be revived with team hp also getting halved(number of plrs\*hp per players= team HP, simpool)
+- dead players(the n/2 not selected to play), act as ghosts, who search and collect resources for the team.
+- on the number of players in team reaching one, the team dies. and is eliminated.
+04 — ghost mode
+- the ghosts will play minigames to earn resources 
+     a clicker game where you need to "CONTRIBUTE" your soul to your comrades(communist game intensifies). To create 
+04 — Ghost Mode
+- Players who aren't selected for revival become ghosts.
+- Ghosts continue contributing to their team's survival.
+- Ghosts can collect resources for their team.
+- Ghosts can play minigames to generate resources.
+- Different resource types have different minigames.
+- Ghosts can choose which resource they want to contribute.
+05 — Soul Contribution
+- Soul is a resource generated by ghosts through a clicker minigame.
+- Ghosts click the central soul sprite to contribute their souls to their comrades.
+- Souls are converted into HP and added to the team's health pool.
+- The clicking rate (CPS) is measured over a rolling 10-second window.
+- At 5 CPS or more, a ghost generates 0.2 × CPS HP/s; exactly 5 CPS is 1 HP/s.
+- Below 5 CPS, team HP drops by 0.2 HP/s.
+- Generated HP is banked until living players need it, with no maximum cap.
+- The HP bar is red below 100 × living teammates, yellow up to that baseline + 90, and green above.
+- Random shapes appear every 10–30 seconds; clicking one grants +5 HP.
+- Ghost-generated HP is used when living players have low health.
+- Q pauses and resumes the minigame.
+Health regeneration rules
+- Teams without ghosts regenerate HP normally.
+- Normal regeneration happens at a fixed rate.
+- Normal regeneration does not happen instantly.
+- Teams with ghosts cannot regenerate HP normally.
+- Teams with ghosts depend on ghost-generated HP instead.
+06 — Bullet Press
+- Follow keyboard instructions to create magazines.
+- One sequence fills one magazine; a sequence is 10 characters drawn from A–Z, typed in order.
+- A correct key fills the next slot green; a wrong key fills it red and marks the whole magazine broken.
+- A broken magazine cannot be discarded; it stays until all 10 slots are filled, then produces nothing.
+- The entire sequence must be completed for the next magazine run.
+- A perfect magazine produces magazines equal to the multiplier.
+- The multiplier is a whole number from 1x to 5x based on average time per key: ≤400 ms is x5, ≤600 ms x4, ≤800 ms x3, ≤1100 ms x2, slower x1.
+- A broken magazine awards x0 and increments the broken count.
+- Finished magazines eject to the right and the next empty magazine enters from the left.
+- takeMagazines() returns whole magazines to the team.
+07 — stewedluck:
+-on every kill by the live players, the team gets killmit. which can be used to spin a stew pot to get any special powerup in the game.(communist engineering)
+08-dash supplement factory
+- A station-management factory game that produces dash seconds.
+- Take empty pots from the unlimited intake and drag them through twelve numbered processes over six reusable stations.
+- Each station holds four pots in a 2×2 grid and processes automatically; a pot must be moved before its ready window expires or it spoils and frees its slot.
+- A pot can only enter the station for its current process; wrong-station drops are rejected for free.
+- Process 12 must finish before the bottler accepts the pot.
+- The bottler holds four pots and empties them one at a time over ten seconds each; waiting pots queue.
+- One pot produces 20 vials; one vial is one second of dash.
+- Vials ride the upper outfeed belt as the pot drains; the emptied pot rides the lower belt away.
+- takeVials() / takeSeconds() return the produced dash to the team.
\ No newline at end of file

1: # commieD-Idea

4: - Multiplayer maze shooter (browser-based)

15: 02actual gameplay
16: - the entire game happens in a limited procedurally generated maze.
17: - each team begins in a large empty space surrounded by the maze. which is its spawn point. enemies cannot enter here. and spawn vulnerability is also here. 
18: - the whole game is top down 2d.
19: - the screen will consist of the following:
20: .top left=minimap which will either show the nearby area, minimap of the whole maze or a list of your teammates which you can quickly select from by using arrow keys to get the route to them.(these different modes can be switched by using x key)
21: .top center=performance and connection stats. strip format. with colour change for good, acceptable and bad stat values.
22: .bottom left=team reserves(ammo, dash, current powerup)
23: .bottom center=team health
24: .top right=comrats(stats of your comrades)

26: - special items are found around the world as collectibles strewn around in mazes
27: - the maze itself is a tiled system containing the following types of tiles: wall, free space,. this is saved and used by program with the help of a 1d array whose scale is fixed at a square for now.
28: - for ghosts, they can immediately switch between different tasks by using 1,2,3(clicker,ammo,dash)
29: - special items that can be triggered with space are 1 time and everyone gets the special item. in case more are collected, they become the current special item and the item before it is saved and becomes current item again when special items is empty. anybody can use this item and exhaust it for the entire team.(communism at peak).
30: - special items cannot be stacked. one can only be triggered when there are no current effects. effects are applied teamwide when triggered.
31: -special items can randomly spawn on any free 
32: 03maze design:
33: - the maze is a structure with large spaces for the team spawn points.
34: - it will be a 3 pass system. first randomly set the spawn areas fo the different teams, then generate the maze then at last generate the special items.
35: - each tile is the same size as the player. the player is a circle with 1 pointed end. acting as gunpoint.
36: - the spawnpoint area is calculated as the sum of all the tiles per player(2 tiles per player). converted into a rectangle where the length and breadth have least difference.

Let me write it.

```js
export const TILE = Object.freeze({ FREE: 0, WALL: 1 });

export const MAZE_DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({
  width: 61,
  height: 61,
  teams: 2,
  playersPerTeam: 8,
  itemsPerTeam: 4,
  seed: 1,
});

export function mulberry32(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return function next() {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

export function makeRng(seed) {
  const next = mulberry32(seed);
  return {
    next,
    int(max) { return Math.floor(next() * max); },
    pick(list) { return list[this.int(list.length)]; },
    shuffle(list) { for (let i = list.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = this.int(i + 1); [list[i], list[j]] = [list[j], list[i]]; } return list; },
  };
}

export function bestRectangle(area) {
  if (!Number.isInteger(area) || area <= 0) throw new RangeError('area must be a positive integer');
  let best = null;
  for (let w = 1; w * w <= area; w += 1) {
    if (area % w !== 0) continue;
    const h = area / w;
    const diff = h - w;
    if (best === null || diff < best.diff) best = { width: h, height: w, diff };
  }
  return { width: best.width, height: best.height };
}
```
For area 16: w=1 h16 diff15; w2 h8 diff6; w4 h4 diff0 → 4x4. Good.

Spawn placement:
```js
function rectsOverlap(a, b, pad = 0) {
  return a.x - pad < b.x + b.width &&
         a.x + a.width + pad > b.x &&
         a.y - pad < b.y + b.height &&
         a.y + a.height + pad > b.y;
}
```
Need spawns placed with margin 1 from walls/edges. Map borders stay walls.

```js
function placeSpawnZones(rng, width, height, teams, playersPerTeam) {
  const area = Math.max(2, playersPerTeam * 2);
  const { width: rw, height: rh } = bestRectangle(area);
  const zones = [];
  const attempts = 400;
  for (let t = 0; t < teams; t++) {
    let placed = null;
    for (let i = 0; i < attempts && !placed; i++) {
      const x = 1 + rng.int(Math.max(1, width - rw - 1));
      const y = 1 + rng.int(Math.max(1, height - rh - 1));
      const rect = { x, y, width: rw, height: rh };
      if (x + rw >= width - 1 || y + rh >= height - 1) continue;
      if (zones.some((z) => rectsOverlap(rect, z, 2))) continue;
      placed = rect;
    }
    if (!placed) throw new Error('map too small for spawn zones');
    zones.push({ teamId: t, ...placed });
  }
  return zones;
}
```
Random position formula: x in [1, width-rw-2] inclusive. `1 + rng.int(width - rw - 1)` gives max `1 + (width-rw-2) = width-rw-1`. Then x+rw = width-1, which is border wall. We want x+rw <= width-2, so max x = width-rw-2. So `1 + rng.int(width - rw - 2)` (if positive) gives 1..width-rw-2. Let me use `const maxX = width - rw - 2; if (maxX < 1) ...; x = 1 + rng.int(maxX)`. Hmm `rng.int(n)` gives 0..n-1. So x max = 1 + (maxX-1) = maxX = width-rw-2. Good. Similarly y.

Map size check: area of map minus border. Ensure enough.

Maze carve:
```js
function carveMaze(rng, tiles, width, height) {
  const idx = (x, y) => y * width + x;
  // start at (1,1)
  const startX = 1, startY = 1;
  tiles[idx(startX,startY)] = TILE.FREE;
  const stack = [[startX, startY]];
  const dirs = [[0,-2],[0,2],[-2,0],[2,0]];
  while (stack.length) {
    const [x, y] = stack[stack.length-1];
    const options = [];
    for (const [dx, dy] of dirs) {
      const nx = x+dx, ny = y+dy;
      if (nx <= 0 || ny <= 0 || nx >= width-1 || ny >= height-1) continue;
      if (tiles[idx(nx,ny)] === TILE.WALL) options.push([nx,ny,dx,dy]);
    }
    if (!options.length) { stack.pop(); continue; }
    const [nx,ny,dx,dy] = options[rng.int(options.length)];
    tiles[idx(x+dx/2, y+dy/2)] = TILE.FREE;
    tiles[idx(nx,ny)] = TILE.FREE;
    stack.push([nx,ny]);
  }
}
```
This carves on odd cells. Width/height should be odd so cells cover to edge-1. Use 61.

class Team {
  constructor(id, name, spawn, color) {

    this.hp = 0; this.maxHp = 0; this.ammo = START_AMMO; this.dashSeconds = START_DASH_SECONDS;

Game:
```js
export class Game {
  constructor({ seed=1, teams=2, playersPerTeam=8, width=61, height=61, itemsPerTeam=4, rng } = {}) {
    this.maze = generateMaze({...});
    this.seed = seed;
    this.rng = makeRng(seed ^ 0x9e3779b9);
    this.players = [];
    this.teams = [];
    this.bullets = [];
    this.bombs = [];
    this.events = [];
    this.timeMs = 0;
    this.nextPlayerId = 1;
    this.nextBulletId = 1;
    this.#createTeamsAndPlayers();
  }
}
```
For each team, maxHp = livingPlayers * 100, hp = maxHp. Players spawn at random distinct tiles inside room. Player positions at tile centers (x+0.5). Use rng.

Input processing:
```js
step(dtMs, inputs = new Map()) {
  const dt = Math.max(0, Math.min(dtMs, 100));
  this.timeMs += dt;
  const dtSec = dt / 1000;
  this.events.length = 0; // each step returns events
  // update effects
  for (const team of this.teams) {
    if (!team.activeEffect) continue;
    team.activeEffect.msLeft -= dt;
    if (team.activeEffect.msLeft <= 0) { team.activeEffect = null; this.events.push({type:'effect-ended', teamId: team.id}); }
  }
  // players
  for (const player of this.players) {
    const input = inputs.get(player.id) ?? EMPTY_INPUT;
    if (team.eliminated) continue;
    if (!player.alive) { // ghost
      this.#updateGhost(player, input, dtSec, dt);
      continue;
    }
    this.#updateAlive(player, input, dtSec, dt);
  }
  // bullets
  ...
  // bombs
  ...
  // items pickup for alive or ghost
  ...
  return { events: [...this.events], timeMs: this.timeMs };
}
```

{ up:false, down:false, left:false, right:false, aimX, aimY, shoot:false, dash:false, useSpecial:false, ghostTask:null }
```
aimX/aimY are world coordinates. The client computes from mouse + camera.

Movement collision:
```js
#movePlayer(player, dx, dy) {
  const phaseless = this.#hasEffect(team, 'phaseless');
  // x axis
  const nx = player.x + dx;
  if (phaseless || !this.#circleHitsSolid(player.teamId, nx, player.y)) player.x = nx;
  const ny = player.y + dy;
  if (phaseless || !this.#circleHitsSolid(player.teamId, player.x, ny)) player.y = ny;
}
```
`#circleHitsSolid(teamId, x, y)` checks tiles overlapping circle around (x,y) with radius; wall or enemy spawn.
- For tile range: `minX = Math.floor(x - r)`, `maxX = Math.floor(x + r)`, etc.
- A tile is solid if TILE.WALL, or spawnTeamAt(maze, tx, ty) !== -1 && !== teamId.
- But player's own spawn is free and not solid.

Clamp position to map.

Aim: if aim provided, angle = atan2(aimY - player.y, aimX - player.x). Else angle from movement vector.

Bullet spawn at player pos + direction * (PLAYER_RADIUS + 0.2). Velocity = dir * BULLET_SPEED. `{ id, teamId, x, y, vx, vy, lifeMs, damage }`. Damage based on team effect gunPlus.

Damage:
```js
#damageTeam(teamId, amount, sourceTeamId) {
  const team = this.teams[teamId];
  if (team.eliminated) return;
  team.hp = Math.max(0, team.hp - amount);
  events.push({type:'damage', teamId, amount,...});
  if (team.hp <= 0) this.#downTeam(team, sourceTeamId);
}
```

Down/revive:
```js
#downTeam(team, killerTeamId) {
  const living = this.players.filter(p => p.teamId===team.id && p.alive);
  if (killerTeamId != null && killerTeamId !== team.id) this.teams[killerTeamId].kills += 1;
  for (const p of living) { p.alive=false; p.isGhost=true; p.hp? no; }
  this.events.push({type:'team-downed', teamId: team.id});
  if (living.length <= 2) { // n/2 would be <=1
    team.eliminated = true;
    team.hp = 0;
    this.events.push({type:'team-eliminated', teamId: team.id});
    return;
  }
  const keepCount = Math.floor(living.length / 2);
  // choose random keep from living
  const shuffled = this.rng.shuffle([...living]);
  const kept = shuffled.slice(0, keepCount);
  const ghosts = shuffled.slice(keepCount);
  for (const p of kept) { p.alive=true; p.isGhost=false; }
  for (const p of ghosts) { p.alive=false; p.isGhost=true; }
  team.maxHp = keepCount * TEAM_HP_PER_PLAYER;
  team.hp = Math.round(team.maxHp * 0.5);
  // reposition to spawn
  ...
  this.events.push({type:'team-revived', teamId: team.id, living: keepCount, ghosts: ghosts.length});
}

Special use:
```js
useSpecial(teamId, playerId) {
  const team = this.teams[teamId];
  if (team.eliminated) return { used:false, reason:'eliminated' };
  if (team.activeEffect) return { used:false, reason:'effect-active' };
  const type = team.specials[team.specials.length-1];
  if (!type) return { used:false, reason:'empty' };
  team.specials.pop();
  switch(type) {
    case 'bomb': spawn bomb from player toward angle; break;
    case 'gunPlus': team.activeEffect = {type, msLeft: 120000}; break;
    case 'phaseless': team.activeEffect = {type, msLeft: 20000}; break;
    case 'coat': team.activeEffect = {type, msLeft: 20000}; break;
  }
  event...
  return { used:true, type, current: team.specials.at(-1) ?? null };
}

`directionFor(angle)` mapping:
```js
const oct = Math.round(angle / OCT);
const dir = { x: Math.cos(oct*OCT), y: Math.sin(oct*OCT) };
return { right: dir.x > 0.4, left: dir.x < -0.4, down: dir.y > 0.4, up: dir.y < -0.4 };
```
Since oct gives 8 dirs with components 0, ±0.707, ±1. Threshold 0.4 works.

`safeAngle(game, bot, desired)`:
```js
const offsets = [0, OCT, -OCT, 2*OCT, -2*OCT, 3*OCT, -3*OCT, 4*OCT];
for (offset of offsets) {
  const angle = desired + offset;
  const nx = bot.x + Math.cos(angle)*0.9;
  const ny = bot.y + Math.sin(angle)*0.9;
  if (!blocked(game, bot.teamId, nx, ny)) return angle;
}
return desired;
```
`blocked` checks wall or enemy spawn.

fromX;\n  const dy = toY - fromY;\n  const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(distance / 0.2));\n\n  for (let step = 1; step < steps; step += 1) {\n    const x = fromX + (dx * step) / steps;\n    const y = fromY + (dy * step) / steps;\n    if (isWall(game.maze, Math.floor(x), Math.floor(y))) {\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction directionInput(angle) {\n  const octant = Math.round(angle / OCT);\n  const x = Math.cos(octant * OCT);\n  const y = Math.sin(octant * OCT);\n\n  return {\n    right: x > 0.4,\n    left: x < -0.4,\n    down: y > 0.4,\n    up: y < -0.4,\n  };\n}\n\nexport class BotDirector {\n  constructor(game, { seed = 1 } = {}) {\n    this.game = game;\n    this.rng = makeRng(seed >>> 0);\n    this.state = new Map();\n  }\n\n  inputs() {\n    const inputs = new Map();\n\n    for (const player of this.game.players) {\n      if (!player.isBot) {\n        continue;\n      }\n      inputs.set(player.id, this.#inputFor(player));\n    }\n\n    return inputs;\n  }\n\n  #inputFor(player) {\n    const state = this.#stateFor(player);\n    const team = this.game.teams[player.teamId];\n\n    if (!player.alive) {\n      return this.#ghostInput(player, state);\n    }\n\n    const enemies = this.game.players.filter(\n      (other) => other.alive && other.teamId !== player.teamId,\n    );\n\n    let target = null;\n    let targetDistance = Infinity;\n\n    for (const enemy of enemies) {\n      const distance = Math.hypot(enemy.x - player.x, enemy.y - player.y);\n      if (distance < targetDistance) {\n        target = enemy;\n        targetDistance = distance;\n      }\n    }\n\n    let desiredAngle = state.wanderAngle;\n    let shoot = false;\n\n    if (target) {\n      desiredAngle = Math.atan2(target.y - player.y, target.x - player.x);\n      const visible = lineOfSight(this.game, player.x, player.y, target.x, target.y);\n\n      if (visible && targetDistance < 9 && team.ammo > 40 && this.game.timeMs >= state.fireAt) {\n        shoot = true;\n        state.fireAt = this.game.timeMs + 260 + this.rng.next() * 260;\n      }\n\n      if (targetDistance < 2.2) {\n        desiredAngle += Math.PI;\n      } else if (targetDistance <= 4.5) {\n        desiredAngle += state.strafeSign * (Math.PI / 2);\n      }\n    } else {\n      const item = this.#nearestItem(player);\n      if (item) {\n        desiredAngle = Math.atan2(item.y + 0.5 - player.y, item.x + 0.5 - player.x);\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (this.rng.next() < 0.02) {\n      state.strafeSign *= -1;\n      state.wanderAngle = this.rng.next() * Math.PI * 2;\n    }\n\n    const angle = this.#safeAngle(player, desiredAngle);\n    const move = directionInput(angle);\n\n    return {\n      ...EMPTY_INPUT,\n      ...move,\n      aimX: target ? target.x : player.x + Math.cos(angle) * 4,\n      aimY: target ? target.y : player.y + Math.sin(angle) * 4,\n      shoot,\n    };\n  }\n\n  #ghostInput(player, state) {\n    const item = this.#nearestItem(player);\n    let angle = state.wanderAngle;\n\n    if (item) {\n      angle = Math.atan2(item.y + 0.5 - player.y, item.x + 0.5 - player.x);\n    } else if (this.rng.next() < 0.03) {\n      state.wanderAngle = this.rng.next() * Math.PI * 2;\n    }\n\n    if (this.rng.next() < 0.004) {\n      const tasks = ['clicker', 'ammo', 'dash'];\n      state.ghostTask = tasks[this.rng.int(tasks.length)];\n    }\n\n    return {\n      ...EMPTY_INPUT,\n      ...directionInput(angle),\n      ghostTask: state.ghostTask,\n    };\n  }\n\n  #safeAngle(player, desired) {\n    const offsets = [0, OCT, -OCT, OCT * 2, -OCT * 2, OCT * 3, -OCT * 3, Math.PI];\n\n    for (const offset of offsets) {\n      const angle = desired + offset;\n      const x = player.x + Math.cos(angle) * 0.95;\n      const y = player.y + Math.sin(angle) * 0.95;\n      if (!blocked(this.game, player.teamId, x, y)) {\n        return angle;\n      }\n    }\n\n    return desired;\n  }\n\n  #nearestItem(player) {\n    let best = null;\n    let bestDistance = Infinity;\n\n    for (const item of this.game.maze.items) {\n      if (item.taken) {\n        continue;\n      }\n      const distance = Math.hypot(item.x + 0.5 - player.x, item.y + 0.5 - player.y);\n      if (distance < bestDistance) {\n        best = item;\n        bestDistance = distance;\n      }\n    }\n\n    return best;\n  }\n\n  #stateFor(player) {\n    let state = this.state.get(player.id);\n\n    if (!state) {\n      state = {\n        wanderAngle: this.rng.next() * Math.PI * 2,\n        strafeSign: this.rng.next() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1,\n        fireAt: 0,\n        ghostTask: 'clicker',\n      };\n      this.state.set(player.id, state);\n    }\n\n    return state;\n  }\n}\n",
  "path": "/mnt/c/Users/Obhi/Desktop/commieD/main-game/src/bots.js"
}

```js
function buildMazeBitmap() {
  const { width, height, tiles, spawnZones } = game.maze;
  mazeBitmap = document.createElement('canvas');
  mazeBitmap.width = width;
  mazeBitmap.height = height;
  const ctx = mazeBitmap.getContext('2d');
  const image = ctx.createImageData(width, height);
  const data = image.data;
  const teamColors = game.teams.map(t => hexToRgb(t.color));
  for (let i=0;i<tiles.length;i++) {
    const offset = i*4;
    if (tiles[i] === TILE.WALL) {
      data[offset]=54; data[offset+1]=48; data[offset+2]=40; data[offset+3]=255;
    } else {
      const owner = spawnZones[i];
      if (owner >= 0) {
        const c = teamColors[owner];
        data[offset]=c[0]*0.45+12; ... alpha 255
      } else {
        data[offset]=14; data[offset+1]=12; data[offset+2]=10; data[offset+3]=255;
      }
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(image,0,0);
}
```

function aimWorld() {
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const cx = rect.width/2, cy = rect.height/2;
  return {
    x: camera.x + (mouse.x - cx)/TILE_PX,
    y: camera.y + (mouse.y - cy)/TILE_PX,
  };
}

function frame(now) {
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  const dt = Math.min(100, now - lastFrame || 16);
  lastFrame = now;
  if (!document.hidden && game.teams[LOCAL_TEAM] && !paused?) {
    const inputs = bots.inputs();
    const pInput = playerInput();
    if (me) inputs.set(me.id, pInput);
    const result = game.step(dt, inputs);
    handleEvents(result.events);
    specialRequested = false;
  }
  updateCamera(dt);
  render(now);
  updateHud(now);
}

      case 'team-revived': if (event.teamId===me.teamId) toast(`REVIVED ${event.living} COMRADES`, 'good'); break;
      case 'team-eliminated': if (event.teamId===me.teamId) showBanner('ELIMINATED','Press R for a new match'); else toast('ENEMY TEAM ELIMINATED','good'); break;
      case 'special-used': toast(`COMmunal ${label} TRIGGERED`,'good'); break;
    }
  }
}

seed,\n    teams: 2,\n    playersPerTeam: PLAYERS_PER_TEAM,\n    width: 61,\n    height: 61,\n    itemsPerTeam: ITEMS_PER_TEAM,\n  });\n\n  me = game.join({ teamId: LOCAL_TEAM, name: 'YOU' });\n  for (let index = 1; index < PLAYERS_PER_TEAM; index += 1) {\n    game.join({ teamId: LOCAL_TEAM, isBot: true });\n  }\n  for (let index = 0; index < PLAYERS_PER_TEAM; index += 1) {\n    game.join({ teamId: 1, isBot: true });\n  }\n\n  // The local demo keeps a larger reserve than a real match would receive\n  // from the ghost minigames.\n  for (const team of game.teams) {\n    team.ammo = LOCAL_AMMO;\n  }\n\n  bots = new BotDirector(game, { seed: (seed ^ 0x5f3759df) >>> 0 });\n  buildMazeBitmap();\n  camera = { x: me.x, y: me.y };\n  effects = [];\n  mouse.down = false;\n  specialRequested = false;\n  ghostTaskKey = null;\n  ui.banner.hidden = true;\n  comratsNext = 0;\n  hudNext = 0;\n}\n\nfunction buildMazeBitmap() {\n  const { width, height, tiles, spawnZones } = game.maze;\n  mazeBitmap = document.createElement('canvas');\n  mazeBitmap.width = width;\n  mazeBitmap.height = height;\n  const bitmapCtx = mazeBitmap.getContext('2d');\n  const image = bitmapCtx.createImageData(width, height);\n  const data = image.data;\n  const teamColors = game.teams.map((team) => hexToRgb(team.color));\n\n  for (let index = 0; index < tiles.length; index += 1) {\n    const offset = index * 4;\n    const owner = spawnZones[index];\n\n    if (tiles[index] === TILE.WALL) {\n      data[offset] = 56;\n      data[offset + 1] = 49;\n      data[offset + 2] = 39;\n    } else if (owner >= 0) {\n      const color = teamColors[owner];\n      data[offset] = Math.round(12 + color[0] * 0.32);\n      data[offset + 1] = Math.round(10 + color[1] * 0.32);\n      data[offset + 2] = Math.round(8 + color[2] * 0.32);\n    } else {\n      data[offset] = 14;\n      data[offset + 1] = 12;\n      data[offset + 2] = 10;\n    }\n    data[offset + 3] = 255;\n  }\n\n  bitmapCtx.putImageData(image, 0, 0);\n}\n\nfunction hexToRgb(hex) {\n  const value = hex.replace('#', '');\n  return [\n    parseInt(value.slice(0, 2), 16),\n    parseInt(value.slice(2, 4), 16),\n    parseInt(value.slice(4, 6), 16),\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction worldToScreen(x, y) {\n  return [\n    (x - camera.x) * TILE_PX + viewWidth / 2,\n    (y - camera.y) * TILE_PX + viewHeight / 2,\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction screenToWorld(x, y) {\n  return {\n    x: camera.x + (x - viewWidth / 2) / TILE_PX,\n    y: camera.y + (y - viewHeight / 2) / TILE_PX,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction playerInput() {\n  const aim = screenToWorld(mouse.x, mouse.y);\n\n  return {\n    ...EMPTY_INPUT,\n    up: keys.has('KeyW') || keys.has('ArrowUp'),\n    down: keys.has('KeyS') || keys.has('ArrowDown'),\n    left: keys.has('KeyA') || keys.has('ArrowLeft'),\n    right: keys.has('KeyD') || keys.has('ArrowRight'),\n    aimX: aim.x,\n    aimY: aim.y,\n    shoot: mouse.down,\n    dash: keys.has('ShiftLeft') || keys.has('ShiftRight'),\n    useSpecial: specialRequested,\n    ghostTask: ghostTaskKey,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction frame(now) {\n  requestAnimationFrame(frame);\n\n  const delta = lastFrame === 0 ? 16 : Math.min(100, now - lastFrame);\n  lastFrame = now;\n\n  fpsFrames += 1;\n  fpsTime += delta;\n  if (fpsTime >= 500) {\n    fps = (fpsFrames * 1000) / fpsTime;\n    frameMs = fpsTime / fpsFrames;\n    fpsFrames = 0;\n    fpsTime = 0;\n  }\n\n  if (!document.hidden && game) {\n    const inputs = bots.inputs();\n    if (me) {\n      inputs.set(me.id, playerInput());\n    }\n\n    const result = game.step(delta, inputs);\n    handleEvents(result.events);\n    specialRequested = false;\n  }\n\n  if (me) {\n    const follow = me.isGhost ? 0.12 : 0.2;\n    camera.x += (me.x - camera.x) * follow;\n    camera.y += (me.y - camera.y) * follow;\n  }\n\n  render(now);\n  updateHud(now);\n}\n\nfunction handleEvents(events) {\n  for (const event of events) {\n    switch (event.type) {\n      case 'bomb':\n        effects.push({\n          type: 'blast',\n          x: event.x,\n          y: event.y,\n          radius: event.radius,\n          life: 620,\n          total: 620,\n        });\n        toast('BOMB — COMMUNAL DEMOLITION', 'bad');\n        break;\n      case 'shot':\n        effects.push({\n          type: 'flash',\n          x: event.x ?? null,\n          y: event.y ?? null,\n          playerId: event.playerId,\n          life: 90,\n          total: 90,\n        });\n        break;\n      case 'item-collected': {\n        const meta = ITEM_META[event.itemType];\n        if (event.duplicate) {\n          toast(`${meta.name} — ALREADY HELD`);\n        } else if (event.immediate) {\n          toast(`${meta.name} ADDED TO TEAM RESERVES`, 'good');\n        } else {\n          toast(`${meta.name} — PRESS SPACE TO SHARE`, 'good');\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case 'special-used': {\n        const meta = ITEM_META[event.type];\n        toast(`${meta.name} TRIGGERED FOR THE TEAM`, 'good');\n        break;\n      }\n      case 'team-downed': {\n        const team = game.teams[event.teamId];\n        if (event.teamId === me.teamId) {\n          toast('TEAM DOWN — HALF WILL RETURN', 'bad');\n        } else {\n          toast(`${team.name.toUpperCase()} DOWN`, 'good');\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case 'team-revived': {\n        const team = game.teams[event.teamId];\n        if (event.teamId === me.teamId) {\n          toast(`REVIVED ${event.living} COMRADES — ${event.hp} HP`, 'good');\n        } else {\n          toast(`${team.name.toUpperCase()} REVIVED ${event.living}`, 'bad');\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case 'team-eliminated': {\n        const team = game.teams[event.teamId];\n        if (event.teamId === me.teamId) {\n          showBanner('ELIMINATED', 'Press R for a new match');\n        } else {\n          toast(`${team.name.toUpperCase()} ELIMINATED`, 'good');\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case 'effect-ended':\n        if (event.teamId === me.teamId) {\n          toast(`${EFFECT_NAMES[event.effect] ?? event.effect} EXPIRED`);\n        }\n        break;\n      default:\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction showBanner(title, text) {\n  ui.bannerTitle.textContent = title;\n  ui.bannerText.textContent = text;\n  ui.banner.hidden = false;\n}\n\nfunction toast(message, tone = '') {\n  const element = document.createElement('div');\n  element.className = `toast${tone ? ` is-${tone}` : ''}`;\n  element.textContent = message;\n  ui.toasts.append(element);\n\n  while (ui.toasts.children.length > 4) {\n    ui.toasts.firstElementChild.remove();\n  }\n\n  window.setTimeout(() => element.remove(), 2600);\n}\n\n/* ---------- Render ---------- */\n\nfunction render(now) {\n  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, viewWidth, viewHeight);\n  ctx.fillStyle = '#070605';\n  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, viewWidth, viewHeight);\n\n  drawTiles();\n  drawSpawnZones();\n  drawItems(now);\n  drawBombs(now);\n  drawBullets();\n  drawPlayers(now);\n  drawEffects();\n  drawCrosshair();\n\n  if (game.teams[me.teamId].eliminated) {\n    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(6, 4, 4, 0.45)';\n    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, viewWidth, viewHeight);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction drawTiles() {\n  const halfWidth = viewWidth / (2 * TILE_PX) + 1;\n  const halfHeight = viewHeight / (2 * TILE_PX) + 1;\n  const minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camera.x - halfWidth));\n  const maxX = Math.min(game.maze.width - 1, Math.ceil(camera.x + halfWidth));\n  const minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camera.y - halfHeight));\n  const maxY = Math.min(game.maze.height - 1, Math.ceil(camera.y + halfHeight));\n\n  const wallShades = ['#3b3328', '#352e24', '#41382b'];\n\n  for (let y = minY; y <= maxY; y += 1) {\n    for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x += 1) {\n      const [screenX, screenY] = worldToScreen(x, y);\n      const wall = game.maze.tiles[y * game.maze.width + x] === TILE.WALL;\n\n      if (wall) {\n        const shade = wallShades[(x * 31 + y * 17) % wallShades.length];\n        ctx.fillStyle = shade;\n        ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY, TILE_PX + 0.5, TILE_PX + 0.5);\n        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28)';\n        ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY + TILE_PX - 5, TILE_PX + 0.5, 5);\n        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)';\n        ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY, TILE_PX + 0.5, 2);\n      } else {\n        ctx.fillStyle = (x + y) % 2 === 0 ? '#0d0b09' : '#0b0a08';\n        ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY, TILE_PX + 0.5, TILE_PX + 0.5);\n        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.018)';\n        ctx.fillRect(screenX + TILE_PX - 1, screenY, 1, TILE_PX);\n        ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY + TILE_PX - 1, TILE_PX, 1);\n      }\n\n      const owner = spawnTeamAt(game.maze, x, y);\n      if (owner >= 0 && !wall) {\n        ctx.fillStyle = withAlpha(game.teams[owner].color, 0.07);\n        ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY, TILE_PX + 0.5, TILE_PX + 0.5);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction drawSpawnZones() {\n  for (const spawn of game.maze.spawns) {\n    const team = game.teams[spawn.teamId];\n    const [x, y] = worldToScreen(spawn.x, spawn.y);\n    ctx.strokeStyle = withAlpha(team.color, 0.45);\n    ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n    ctx.setLineDash([8, 6]);\n    ctx.strokeRect(x, y, spawn.width * TILE_PX, spawn.height * TILE_PX);\n    ctx.setLineDash([]);\n\n    ctx.fillStyle = withAlpha(team.color, 0.6);\n    ctx.font = '700 9px \"Trebuchet MS\", sans-serif';\n    ctx.textAlign = 'left';\n    ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';\n    ctx.fillText(`${team.name.toUpperCase()} SPAWN`, x + 3, y - 4);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction drawItems(now) {\n  const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(now / 240) * 0.08;\n\n  for (const item of game.maze.items) {\n    if (item.taken) {\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const [x, y] = worldToScreen(item.x + 0.5, item.y + 0.5);\n    if (x < -40 || y < -40 || x > viewWidth + 40 || y > viewHeight + 40) {\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const meta = ITEM_META[item.type];\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(x, y);\n    ctx.scale(pulse, pulse);\n    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8, 6, 5, 0.85)';\n    ctx.strokeStyle = meta.color;\n    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.roundRect(-9, -9, 18, 18, 3);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.fillStyle = meta.color;\n    ctx.font = '700 10px \"Trebuchet MS\", sans-serif';\n    ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n    ctx.fillText(meta.label, 0, 0.5);\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction drawBombs(now) {\n  for (const bomb of game.bombs) {\n    const [x, y] = worldToScreen(bomb.x, bomb.y);\n    const flicker = 0.6 + Math.sin(now / 60) * 0.4;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(x, y, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#2a0f0f';\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255, 90, 80, ${flicker})`;\n    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;\n    ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(x + 4, y - 7, 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#f2c94c';\n    ctx.fill();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction drawBullets() {\n  ctx.lineCap = 'round';\n\n  for (const bullet of game.bullets) {\n    const [x, y] = worldToScreen(bullet.x, bullet.y);\n    const length = Math.hypot(bullet.vx, bullet.vy) || 1;\n    const trailX = (bullet.vx / length) * 10;\n    const trailY = (bullet.vy / length) * 10;\n\n    ctx.strokeStyle = '#f8e7ab';\n    ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(x, y);\n    ctx.lineTo(x - trailX, y - trailY);\n    ctx.stroke();\n\n    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(242, 201, 76, 0.25)';\n    ctx.lineWidth = 5;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(x, y);\n    ctx.lineTo(x - trailX, y - trailY);\n    ctx.stroke();\n  }\n\n  ctx.lineCap = 'butt';\n}\n\nfunction drawPlayers(now) {\n  for (const player of game.players) {\n    const team = game.teams[player.teamId];\n    const [x, y] = worldToScreen(player.x, player.y);\n    const radius = PLAYER_RADIUS * TILE_PX;\n    const isMe = player.id === me.id;\n\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalAlpha = player.alive ? 1 : 0.4;\n\n    if (!player.alive) {\n      ctx.strokeStyle = withAlpha(team.color, 0.8);\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;\n      ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, radius + 4 + Math.sin(now / 300 + player.id) * 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.setLineDash([]);\n    }\n\n    // Gun barrel\n    const gunLength = radius * 1.7;\n    const gunX = Math.cos(player.angle);\n    const gunY = Math.sin(player.angle);\n    const perpX = -gunY;\n    const perpY = gunX;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.moveTo(x + gunX * gunLength, y + gunY * gunLength);\n    ctx.lineTo(x + perpX * 3, y + perpY * 3);\n    ctx.lineTo(x - perpX * 3, y - perpY * 3);\n    ctx.closePath();\n    ctx.fillStyle = player.alive ? '#e9dfc5' : withAlpha('#e9dfc5', 0.5);\n    ctx.fill();\n\n    // Body\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(x, y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fillStyle = team.color;\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.lineWidth = isMe ? 2.5 : 1.5;\n    ctx.strokeStyle = isMe ? '#ffffff' : '#100d09';\n    ctx.stroke();\n\n    if (isMe) {\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, radius + 5, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(242, 201, 76, 0.7)';\n      ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n      ctx.setLineDash([2, 4]);\n      ctx.stroke();\n      ctx.setLineDash([]);\n    }\n\n    if (player.teamId === me.teamId) {\n      ctx.globalAlpha = player.alive ? 0.85 : 0.5;\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#d7cfb9';\n      ctx.font = '700 7.5px \"Trebuchet MS\", sans-serif';\n      ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n      ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';\n      ctx.fillText(isMe ? 'YOU' : player.name.toUpperCase(), x, y - radius - 5);\n    }\n\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction drawEffects() {\n  for (const effect of effects) {\n    const progress = 1 - effect.life / effect.total;\n\n    if (effect.type === 'blast') {\n      const [x, y] = worldToScreen(effect.x, effect.y);\n      const radius = effect.radius * TILE_PX * progress;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(x, y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(242, 120, 60, ${0.28 * (1 - progress)})`;\n      ctx.fill();\n      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255, 210, 140, ${0.8 * (1 - progress)})`;\n      ctx.lineWidth = 3;\n      ctx.stroke();\n    } else if (effect.type === 'flash') {\n      const player = game.playersById.get(effect.playerId);\n      if (!player) {\n        continue;\n      }\n      const [x, y] = worldToScreen(player.x, player.y);\n      const muzzleX = x + Math.cos(player.angle) * PLAYER_RADIUS * TILE_PX * 1.9;\n      const muzzleY = y + Math.sin(player.angle) * PLAYER_RADIUS * TILE_PX * 1.9;\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(muzzleX, muzzleY, 5 * (1 - progress) + 1, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255, 236, 160, ${1 - progress})`;\n      ctx.fill();\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction drawCrosshair() {\n  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(242, 201, 76, 0.55)';\n  ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n  ctx.beginPath();\n  ctx.arc(mouse.x, mouse.y, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n  ctx.stroke();\n  ctx.beginPath();\n  ctx.moveTo(mouse.x - 11, mouse.y);\n  ctx.lineTo(mouse.x - 3, mouse.y);\n  ctx.moveTo(mouse.x + 3, mouse.y);\n  ctx.lineTo(mouse.x + 11, mouse.y);\n  ctx.moveTo(mouse.x, mouse.y - 11);\n  ctx.lineTo(mouse.x, mouse.y - 3);\n  ctx.moveTo(mouse.x, mouse.y + 3);\n  ctx.lineTo(mouse.x, mouse.y + 11);\n  ctx.stroke();\n}\n\n/* ---------- HUD ---------- */\n\nfunction updateHud(now) {\n  updateEffects(now);\n\n  if (now >= hudNext) {\n    hudNext = now + 120;\n    updateStats();\n    updateReserves();\n    updateHealth();\n    updateMinimap();\n  }\n\n  if (now >= comratsNext) {\n    comratsNext = now + 260;\n    updateComrats();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction updateEffects(now) {\n  const delta = lastFrame === 0 ? 16 : Math.min(100, now - lastFrame);\n  effects = effects.filter((effect) => {\n    effect.life -= delta;\n    return effect.life > 0;\n  });\n}\n\nfunction updateStats() {\n  ui.stats.fps.textContent = `FPS ${Math.round(fps)}`;\n  ui.stats.fps.className = `stat ${fps >= 50 ? '' : fps >= 30 ? 'is-ok' : 'is-bad'}`;\n\n  ui.stats.frame.textContent = `${frameMs.toFixed(1)} ms`;\n  ui.stats.frame.className = `stat ${frameMs <= 20 ? '' : frameMs <= 33 ? 'is-ok' : 'is-bad'}`;\n\n  ui.stats.conn.textContent = 'LOCAL';\n  ui.stats.conn.className = 'stat is-dim';\n\n  const aliveA = game.teams[0].eliminated ? 0 : countAlive(0);\n  const aliveB = game.teams[1].eliminated ? 0 : countAlive(1);\n  ui.stats.alive.textContent = `${aliveA} / ${aliveB}`;\n  ui.stats.alive.className = 'stat is-dim';\n}\n\nfunction countAlive(teamId) {\n  return game.players.filter((player) => player.teamId === teamId && player.alive).length;\n}\n\nfunction updateReserves() {\n  const team = game.teams[me.teamId];\n  ui.teamName.textContent = team.name.toUpperCase();\n  ui.ammo.textContent = String(team.ammo);\n  ui.dash.textContent = `${Math.floor(team.dashSeconds)}s`;\n  ui.special.textContent = team.specials.length\n    ? ITEM_META[team.specials[team.specials.length - 1]].name\n    : '—';\n\n  const queue = team.specials.map((type, index) => {\n    const span = document.createElement('span');\n    const current = index === team.specials.length - 1;\n    span.textContent = ITEM_META[type].name;\n    if (current) {\n      span.className = 'is-current';\n    }\n    return span;\n  });\n  ui.specialQueue.replaceChildren(...queue);\n}\n\nfunction updateHealth() {\n  const team = game.teams[me.teamId];\n  const maxHp = Math.max(1, team.maxHp);\n  const ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, team.hp / maxHp));\n\n  ui.healthTeam.textContent = team.name.toUpperCase();\n  ui.hpLabel.textContent = `${Math.round(team.hp)} / ${Math.round(team.maxHp)}`;\n  ui.hpFill.style.width = `${ratio * 100}%`;\n  ui.hpFill.className = `health__fill${\n    ratio <= 0.34 ? ' is-low' : ratio <= 0.67 ? ' is-mid' : ''\n  }`;\n\n  if (team.activeEffect) {\n    const seconds = Math.max(0, team.activeEffect.msLeft / 1000);\n    ui.effectLabel.textContent = `${EFFECT_NAMES[team.activeEffect.type] ?? team.activeEffect.type} ${seconds.toFixed(0)}s`;\n  } else {\n    ui.effectLabel.textContent = 'NO EFFECT';\n  }\n\n  if (team.eliminated) {\n    ui.statusLabel.textContent = 'ELIMINATED';\n    showBanner('ELIMINATED', 'Press R for a new match');\n  } else if (!me.alive) {\n    ui.statusLabel.textContent = `GHOST · ${TASK_NAMES[me.ghostTask] ?? 'CLICKER'}`;\n    ui.banner.hidden = true;\n  } else {\n    ui.statusLabel.textContent = 'LIVE';\n    ui.banner.hidden = true;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction updateComrats() {\n  const teamPlayers = game.players\n    .filter((player) => player.teamId === me.teamId)\n    .sort((a, b) => Number(b.alive) - Number(a.alive) || a.id - b.id);\n\n  const rows = teamPlayers.map((player) => {\n    const item = document.createElement('li');\n    if (player.id === me.id) {\n      item.className = 'is-me';\n    } else if (!player.alive) {\n      item.className = 'is-dead';\n    }\n\n    const name = document.createElement('span');\n    name.textContent = player.id === me.id ? 'YOU' : player.name.toUpperCase();\n\n    const state = document.createElement('span');\n    state.className = `comrat__state ${\n      !player.alive ? 'is-ghost' : 'is-live'\n    }`;\n    state.textContent = player.alive ? 'LIVE' : 'GHOST';\n\n    item.append(name, state);\n    return item;\n  });\n\n  if (game.teams[me.teamId].eliminated) {\n    const item = document.createElement('li');\n    item.className = 'is-dead';\n    const name = document.createElement('span');\n    name.textContent = 'TEAM ELIMINATED';\n    item.append(name);\n    rows.unshift(item);\n  }\n\n  ui.comrats.replaceChildren(...rows);\n  ui.comratsCount.textContent = `${countAlive(me.teamId)} LIVE`;\n}\n\nfunction updateMinimap() {\n  ui.minimapMode.textContent = MINIMAP_MODES[minimapModeIndex];\n  const size = MINIMAP_SIZE;\n  minimapCtx.clearRect(0, 0, size, size);\n\n  if (!mazeBitmap) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const mode = MINIMAP_MODES[minimapModeIndex];\n\n  if (mode === 'COMRATS') {\n    drawTeamMap();\n    return;\n  }\n\n  let sourceX = 0;\n  let sourceY = 0;\n  let sourceWidth = game.maze.width;\n  let sourceHeight = game.maze.height;\n\n  if (mode === 'NEARBY') {\n    const span = 25;\n    sourceWidth = span;\n    sourceHeight = span;\n    sourceX = clamp(me.x - span / 2, 0, game.maze.width - span);\n    sourceY = clamp(me.y - span / 2, 0, game.maze.height - span);\n  }\n\n  minimapCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n  minimapCtx.drawImage(\n    mazeBitmap,\n    sourceX,\n    sourceY,\n    sourceWidth,\n    sourceHeight,\n    0,\n    0,\n    size,\n    size,\n  );\n\n  const project = (x, y) => [\n    ((x - sourceX) / sourceWidth) * size,\n    ((y - sourceY) / sourceHeight) * size,\n  ];\n\n  for (const item of game.maze.items) {\n    if (item.taken) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    const [x, y] = project(item.x + 0.5, item.y + 0.5);\n    minimapCtx.fillStyle = '#f2c94c';\n    minimapCtx.fillRect(x - 1, y - 1, 2, 2);\n  }\n\n  for (const player of game.players) {\n    const [x, y] = project(player.x, player.y);\n    const team = game.teams[player.teamId];\n    minimapCtx.globalAlpha = player.alive ? 1 : 0.45;\n    minimapCtx.beginPath();\n    minimapCtx.arc(x, y, player.id === me.id ? 2.6 : 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    minimapCtx.fillStyle = team.color;\n    minimapCtx.fill();\n    if (player.id === me.id) {\n      minimapCtx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';\n      minimapCtx.lineWidth = 1;\n      minimapCtx.stroke();\n    }\n  }\n  minimapCtx.globalAlpha = 1;\n\n  if (mode === 'FULL') {\n    const viewTilesX = viewWidth / TILE_PX;\n    const viewTilesY = viewHeight / TILE_PX;\n    const [x, y] = project(camera.x - viewTilesX / 2, camera.y - viewTilesY / 2);\n    minimapCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35)';\n    minimapCtx.lineWidth = 1;\n    minimapCtx.strokeRect(\n      x,\n      y,\n      (viewTilesX / sourceWidth) * size,\n      (viewTilesY / sourceHeight) * size,\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nfunction drawTeamMap() {\n  const size = MINIMAP_SIZE;\n  minimapCtx.fillStyle = '#0b0906';\n  minimapCtx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);\n\n  const rows = game.players\n    .filter((player) => player.teamId === me.teamId)\n    .sort((a, b) => Number(b.alive) - Number(a.alive) || a.id - b.id);\n\n  minimapCtx.font = '8px \"Trebuchet MS\", sans-serif';\n  minimapCtx.textAlign = 'left';\n  minimapCtx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n\n  rows.forEach((player, index) => {\n    const y = 20 + index * 20;\n    minimapCtx.fillStyle = player.alive ? '#d7cfb9' : '#5f5a4c';\n    const label = player.id === me.id ? 'YOU' : player.name.toUpperCase();\n    minimapCtx.fillText(label, 10, y);\n\n    minimapCtx.fillStyle = player.alive ? '#7fc96a' : '#5bc0be';\n    minimapCtx.textAlign = 'right';\n    minimapCtx.fillText(\n      player.alive ? 'LIVE' : `GHOST · ${TASK_NAMES[player.ghostTask] ?? 'CLICKER'}`,\n      size - 10,\n      y,\n    );\n    minimapCtx.textAlign = 'left';\n  });\n\n  minimapCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(242, 201, 76, 0.2)';\n  minimapCtx.beginPath();\n  minimapCtx.moveTo(8, 28);\n  minimapCtx.lineTo(size - 8, 28);\n  minimapCtx.stroke();\n}\n\nfunction clamp(value, min, max) {\n  return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));\n}\n\nfunction withAlpha(hex, alpha) {\n  const [r, g, b] = hexToRgb(hex);\n  return `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, ${alpha})`;\n}\n\n/* ---------- Input ---------- */\n\nwindow.addEventListener('resize', resize);\n\nwindow.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {\n  if (['Space', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight'].includes(event.code)) {\n    event.preventDefault();\n  }\n\n  keys.add(event.code);\n\n  if (event.code === 'Space') {\n    specialRequested = true;\n  } else if (event.code === 'KeyX') {\n    minimapModeIndex = (minimapModeIndex + 1) % MINIMAP_MODES.length;\n  } else if (event.code === 'KeyR') {\n    startMatch(Math.floor(Math.random() * 1_000_000_000));\n  } else if (event.code === 'Digit1') {\n    ghostTaskKey = 'clicker';\n  } else if (event.code === 'Digit2') {\n    ghostTaskKey = 'ammo';\n  } else if (event.code === 'Digit3') {\n    ghostTaskKey = 'dash';\n  }\n});\n\nwindow.addEventListener('keyup', (event) => {\n  keys.delete(event.code);\n});\n\nwindow.addEventListener('blur', () => {\n  keys.clear();\n  mouse.down = false;\n});\n\ncanvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (event) => {\n  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();\n  mouse.x = event.clientX - rect.left;\n  mouse.y = event.clientY - rect.top;\n});\n\ncanvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (event) => {\n  if (event.button === 0) {\n    mouse.down = true;\n  }\n});\n\nwindow.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {\n  mouse.down = false;\n});\n\ncanvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', (event) => event.preventDefault());\n\nwindow.addEventListener('contextmenu', (event) => {\n  if (event.target === canvas) {\n    event.preventDefault();\n  }\n});\n\n/* ---------- Boot ---------- */\n\nresize();\nstartMatch(20261110);\nrequestAnimationFrame(frame);\n",

1: # commieD-Idea
2: 
3: 01main requisites
4: - Multiplayer maze shooter (browser-based)
5: - Large scale: 1000s of concurrent players across 200 cities globally
6: - Goal: be the last team standing
7: - the entire game is minimal in design and assets with the theme of communism(cause commieD=comm(unist)i(com)eD(y))
8: - Teams are divided uniformly.
9: - Teams share everything(from ammo, dashes, and even HP)
10: - If the team HP goes to 0, the entire team dies.
11: - A team is revived with only n/2 of the number of players who were alive in the previous run.
12: - the players who get to live is decided by a random player in the team.
13: - dead players(the n/2 not selected to play), act as ghosts, who search and collect resources for the team.
14: - on the number of players in team reaching one, the team dies. and is eliminated.
15: 02actual gameplay
16: - the entire game happens in a limited procedurally generated maze.
17: - each team begins in a large empty space surrounded by the maze. which is its spawn point. enemies cannot enter here. and spawn vulnerability is also here. 
18: - the whole game is top down 2d.
19: - the screen will consist of the following:
20: .top left=minimap which will either show the nearby area, minimap of the whole maze or a list of your teammates which you can quickly select from by using arrow keys to get the route to them.(these different modes can be switched by using x key)
21: .top center=performance and connection stats. strip format. with colour change for good, acceptable and bad stat values.
22: .bottom left=team reserves(ammo, dash, current powerup)
23: .bottom center=team health
24: .top right=comrats(stats of your comrades)
25: - you move around with wasd and arrowkeys, shift to dash. mouse to aim and click to shoot. hold click to for continuous fire. space for using special item
26: - special items are found around the world as collectibles strewn around in mazes
27: - the maze itself is a tiled system containing the following types of tiles: wall, free space,. this is saved and used by program with the help of a 1d array whose scale is fixed at a square for now.
28: - for ghosts, they can immediately switch between different tasks by using 1,2,3(clicker,ammo,dash)
29: - special items that can be triggered with space are 1 time and everyone gets the special item. in case more are collected, they become the current special item and the item before it is saved and becomes current item again when special items is empty. anybody can use this item and exhaust it for the entire team.(communism at peak).
30: - special items cannot be stacked. one can only be triggered when there are no current effects. effects are applied teamwide when triggered.
31: -special items can randomly spawn on any free 
32: 03maze design:
33: - the maze is a structure with large spaces for the team spawn points.
34: - it will be a 3 pass system. first randomly set the spawn areas fo the different teams, then generate the maze then at last generate the special items.
35: - each tile is the same size as the player. the player is a circle with 1 pointed end. acting as gunpoint.
36: - the spawnpoint area is calculated as the sum of all the tiles per player(2 tiles per player). converted into a rectangle where the length and breadth have least difference.

15:      a clicker game where you need to "CONTRIBUTE" your soul to your comrades(communist game intensifies). To create 
16: 04 — Ghost Mode
17: - Players who aren't selected for revival become ghosts.
18: - Ghosts continue contributing to their team's survival.
19: - Ghosts can collect resources for their team.
20: - Ghosts can play minigames to generate resources.
21: - Different resource types have different minigames.
22: - Ghosts can choose which resource they want to contribute.
23: 05 — Soul Contribution
24: - Soul is a resource generated by ghosts through a clicker minigame.
25: - Ghosts click the central soul sprite to contribute their souls to their comrades.
26: - Souls are converted into HP and added to the team's health pool.
27: - The clicking rate (CPS) is measured over a rolling 10-second window.
28: - At 5 CPS or more, a ghost generates 0.2 × CPS HP/s; exactly 5 CPS is 1 HP/s.
29: - Below 5 CPS, team HP drops by 0.2 HP/s.
30: - Generated HP is banked until living players need it, with no maximum cap.
31: - The HP bar is red below 100 × living teammates, yellow up to that baseline + 90, and green above.
32: - Random shapes appear every 10–30 seconds; clicking one grants +5 HP.
33: - Ghost-generated HP is used when living players have low health.
34: - Q pauses and resumes the minigame.
35: Health regeneration rules
36: - Teams without ghosts regenerate HP normally.
37: - Normal regeneration happens at a fixed rate.
38: - Normal regeneration does not happen instantly.
39: - Teams with ghosts cannot regenerate HP normally.
40: - Teams with ghosts depend on ghost-generated HP instead.
41: 06 — Bullet Press
42: - Follow keyboard instructions to create magazines.
43: - One sequence fills one magazine; a sequence is 10 characters drawn from A–Z, typed in order.
44: - A correct key fills the next slot green; a wrong key fills it red and marks the whole magazine broken.
45: - A broken magazine cannot be discarded; it stays until all 10 slots are filled, then produces nothing.
46: - The entire sequence must be completed for the next magazine run.
47: - A perfect magazine produces magazines equal to the multiplier.
48: - The multiplier is a whole number from 1x to 5x based on average time per key: ≤400 ms is x5, ≤600 ms x4, ≤800 ms x3, ≤1100 ms x2, slower x1.
49: - A broken magazine awards x0 and increments the broken count.
50: - Finished magazines eject to the right and the next empty magazine enters from the left.
51: - takeMagazines() returns whole magazines to the team.
52: 07 --speed dash supplement factory:
53: - A station-management factory game that produces dash seconds.
54: - Take empty pots from the unlimited intake and drag them through twelve numbered processes over six reusable stations.
55: - Each station holds four pots in a 2×2 grid and processes automatically; a pot must be moved before its ready window expires or it spoils and frees its slot.
56: - A pot can only enter the station for its current process; wrong-station drops are rejected for free.
57: - Process 12 must finish before the bottler accepts the pot.
58: - The bottler holds four pots and empties them one at a time over ten seconds each; waiting pots queue.
59: - One pot produces 20 vials; one vial is one second of dash.
60: - Vials ride the upper outfeed belt as the pot drains; the emptied pot rides the lower belt away.
61: - takeVials() / takeSeconds() return the produced dash to the team.

229:     const team = this.teams[teamId];
230:     if (!team || team.eliminated) {
231:       return { used: false, reason: 'eliminated' };
232:     }
233:     if (team.activeEffect) {
234:       return { used: false, reason: 'effect-active' };
235:     }
236: 
237:     const type = team.specials[team.specials.length - 1];
238:     if (!type) {
239:       return { used: false, reason: 'empty' };
240:     }
241: 
242:     const player = this.playersById.get(playerId);
243:     if (!player || !player.alive) {
244:       return { used: false, reason: 'not-alive' };
245:     }
246: 
247:     team.specials.pop();
248:     const result = { used: true, type, current: team.specials.at(-1) ?? null };
249: 
250:     if (type === 'bomb') {
251:       const direction = { x: Math.cos(player.angle), y: Math.sin(player.angle) };
252:       this.bombs.push({
253:         id: this.nextProjectileId,
254:         teamId,
255:         x: player.x + direction.x * (PLAYER_RADIUS + 0.15),
256:         y: player.y + direction.y * (PLAYER_RADIUS + 0.15),
257:         vx: direction.x * BOMB_SPEED,
258:         vy: direction.y * BOMB_SPEED,
259:         lifeMs: BOMB_FUSE_MS,
260:       });
261:       this.nextProjectileId += 1;
262:     } else if (SPECIAL_DURATIONS[type]) {
263:       team.activeEffect = { type, msLeft: SPECIAL_DURATIONS[type] };
264:     }
265: 
266:     return result;
267:   }

323:       .sort((a, b) => {

346:   #placeAtSpawn(player) {

353:       const occupied = this.players.some(

356:           Math.abs(other.y - (y + 0.5)) < 0.6,

366:     player.y = room.y + room.height / 2;

369:   #stepAlive(player, team, input, dt, dtSec, events) {
370:     player.dashMs = Math.max(0, player.dashMs - dt);
371:     player.fireCooldownMs = Math.max(0, player.fireCooldownMs - dt);
372: 
373:     const move = {
374:       x: (input.right ? 1 : 0) - (input.left ? 1 : 0),
375:       y: (input.down ? 1 : 0) - (input.up ? 1 : 0),
376:     };
377:     const moving = move.x !== 0 || move.y !== 0;
378: 
379:     if (input.dash && player.dashMs <= 0 && team.dashSeconds >= DASH_COST_SECONDS) {

383:     }
384: 
385:     let speed = player.dashMs > 0 ? DASH_SPEED : MOVE_SPEED;

389:

400:       player.angle = Math.atan2(move.y, move.x);

403:     if (input.useSpecial) {

416:   #stepGhost(player, input, dt, dtSec, events) {

420:         events.push({

440:   #movePlayer(player, dx, dy) {

444:     if (phaseless) {

461:   #circleHitsSolid(teamId, x, y) {

463:     const maxX = Math.floor(x + PLAYER_RADIUS);

467:     for (let tileY = minY; tileY <= maxY; tileY += 1) {

485:     const gunPlus = this.#hasEffect(team, 'gunPlus');

494:       vx: direction.x * BULLET_SPEED,

510:   #stepBullets(dt, dtSec, events) {

513:     for (const bullet of this.bullets) {
514:       bullet.lifeMs -= dt;
515: 
516:       if (bullet.lifeMs <= 0) {
517:         continue;
518:       }
519: 
520:       const distance = Math.hypot(bullet.vx, bullet.vy) * dtSec;
521:       const substeps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(distance / 0.2));
522:       let removed = false;
523: 
524:       for (let step = 0; step < substeps; step += 1) {
525:         bullet.x += (bullet.vx * dtSec) / substeps;
526:         bullet.y += (bullet.vy * dtSec) / substeps;
527: 
528:         if (this.#projectileHitsSolid(bullet.teamId, bullet.x, bullet.y)) {
529:           events.push({ type: 'bullet-wall', teamId: bullet.teamId });
530:           removed = true;
531:           break;
532:         }
533: 
534:         const victim = this.#enemyPlayerAt(
535:           bullet.teamId,
536:           bullet.x,
537:           bullet.y,
538:           BULLET_RADIUS,
539:         );
540: 
541:         if (victim) {
542:           this.#damageTeam(victim.teamId, bullet.damage, bullet.teamId, events);
543:           events.push({
544:             type: 'bullet-hit',
545:             teamId: victim.teamId,
546:             sourceTeamId: bullet.teamId,
547:             playerId: victim.id,
548:             damage: bullet.damage,
549:           });
550:           removed = true;
551:           break;
552:         }
553:       }
554: 
555:       if (!removed) {
556:         survivors.push(bullet);

562: 
563:   #stepBombs(dt, dtSec, events) {
564:     const survivors = [];
565: 
566:     for (const bomb of this.bombs) {
567:       bomb.lifeMs -= dt;
568: 
569:       const distance = Math.hypot(bomb.vx, bomb.vy) * dtSec;
570:       const substeps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(distance / 0.2));
571:       let exploded = false;
572: 
573:       for (let step = 0; step < substeps; step += 1) {
574:         bomb.x += (bomb.vx * dtSec) / substeps;
575:         bomb.y += (bomb.vy * dtSec) / substeps;
576: 
577:         const hitWall = this.#projectileHitsSolid(bomb.teamId, bomb.x, bomb.y);
578:         const hitEnemy = this.#enemyPlayerAt(bomb.teamId, bomb.x, bomb.y, 0.35);
