584:         }
585:       }
586: 
587:       if (exploded) {
588:         continue;
589:       }
590: 
591:       if (bomb.lifeMs <= 0) {
592:         this.#explodeBomb(bomb, events);
593:         continue;
594:       }
595: 
596:       survivors.push(bomb);
597:     }
598: 
599:     this.bombs = survivors;
600:   }
601: 
602:   #explodeBomb(bomb, events) {
603:     const hitTeams = new Set();
604: 
605:     for (const player of this.players) {
606:       if (player.teamId === bomb.teamId || !player.alive) {
607:         continue;
608:       }
609:       if (hitTeams.has(player.teamId)) {
610:         continue;
611:       }
612:       if (Math.hypot(player.x - bomb.x, player.y - bomb.y) <= BOMB_RADIUS_TILES) {
613:         hitTeams.add(player.teamId);
614:       }
615:     }
616: 
617:     events.push({
618:       type: 'bomb',
619:       teamId: bomb.teamId,
620:       x: bomb.x,
621:       y: bomb.y,
622:       radius: BOMB_RADIUS_TILES,
623:       hitTeams: [...hitTeams],
624:     });
625: 
626:     for (const teamId of hitTeams) {

628:     }

630: 
631:   #projectileHitsSolid(teamId, x, y) {
632:     const tileX = Math.floor(x);
633:     const tileY = Math.floor(y);
634: 
635:     if (isWall(this.maze, tileX, tileY)) {
636:       return true;
637:     }
638: 
639:     const owner = spawnTeamAt(this.maze, tileX, tileY);
640:     return owner !== -1 && owner !== teamId;
641:   }
642: 
643:   #enemyPlayerAt(teamId, x, y, extraRadius) {
644:     for (const player of this.players) {
645:       if (!player.alive || player.teamId === teamId) {
646:         continue;
647:       }
648:       if (Math.hypot(player.x - x, player.y - y) <= PLAYER_RADIUS + extraRadius) {
649:         return player;
650:       }

652:     return null;
653:   }
654: 
655:   #collectItems(events) {
656:     for (const item of this.maze.items) {

658:         continue;
659:       }
660: 
661:       for (const player of this.players) {
662:         const team = this.teams[player.teamId];
663:         if (team.eliminated) {
664:           continue;
665:         }
666: 
667:         if (Math.hypot(player.x - (item.x + 0.5), player.y - (item.y + 0.5)) > PICKUP_RADIUS) {
668:           continue;
669:         }
670: 
671:         item.taken = true;
672:         const result = this.#applyPickup(team, item.type);
673:         events.push({
674:           type: 'item-collected',
675:           teamId: team.id,
676:           playerId: player.id,
677:           itemType: item.type,

685:   #applyPickup(team, itemType) {
686:     if (itemType === 'hp') {
687:       team.hp += 300;
688:       return { immediate: true };
689:     }
690:     if (itemType === 'speed') {
691:       team.dashSeconds += 40;
692:       return { immediate: true };
693:     }
694: 
695:     if (team.specials.includes(itemType)) {
696:       return { duplicate: true };
697:     }
698: 
699:     team.specials.push(itemType);
700:     return { immediate: false, current: team.specials.at(-1) };
701:   }
702: 
703:   #damageTeam(teamId, amount, sourceTeamId, events) {
704:     const team = this.teams[teamId];
705:     if (!team || team.eliminated || team.hp <= 0) {
706:       return;
707:     }
708: 
709:     team.hp = Math.max(0, team.hp - amount);
710:     events.push({
711:       type: 'damage',
712:       teamId,
713:       sourceTeamId,
714:       amount,
715:       hp: team.hp,
716:     });
717: 
718:     if (team.hp <= 0) {
719:       this.#downTeam(team, sourceTeamId, events);
720:     }
721:   }
722: 
723:   #downTeam(team, sourceTeamId, events) {
724:     const living = this.players.filter(
725:       (player) => player.teamId === team.id && player.alive,
726:     );
727: 
728:     if (sourceTeamId !== undefined && sourceTeamId !== team.id) {
729:       this.teams[sourceTeamId].kills += 1;
730:     }
731: 
732:     for (const player of living) {
733:       player.alive = false;
734:       player.isGhost = true;
735:       player.dashMs = 0;
736:     }
737: 
738:     events.push({ type: 'team-downed', teamId: team.id, wasAlive: living.length });
739: 
740:     if (living.length <= 2) {
741:       team.eliminated = true;
742:       team.hp = 0;
743:       team.activeEffect = null;
744:       events.push({ type: 'team-eliminated', teamId: team.id });
745:       return;
746:     }
747: 
748:     const keepCount = Math.floor(living.length / 2);
749:     const shuffled = this.rng.shuffle([...living]);
750:     const kept = shuffled.slice(0, keepCount);
751: 
752:     for (const player of kept) {
753:       player.alive = true;
754:       player.isGhost = false;
755:       player.dashMs = 0;
756:       this.#placeAtSpawn(player);
757:     }
758: 
759:     team.maxHp = keepCount * TEAM_HP_PER_PLAYER;
760:     team.hp = Math.round(team.maxHp * 0.5);
761:     team.activeEffect = null;
762: 
763:     events.push({
764:       type: 'team-revived',
765:       teamId: team.id,
766:       living: keepCount,
767:       ghosts: living.length - keepCount,
768:       hp: team.hp,
769:       maxHp: team.maxHp,
770:     });
771:   }
772: 
773:   #tickEffect(team, dt, events) {
774:     if (!team.activeEffect) {
775:       return;
776:     }
777: 
778:     team.activeEffect.msLeft -= dt;
779: 
780:     if (team.activeEffect.msLeft <= 0) {
781:       const type = team.activeEffect.type;
782:       team.activeEffect = null;
783:       events.push({ type: 'effect-ended', teamId: team.id, effect: type });
784:     }
785:   }
786: 
787:   #hasEffect(team, type) {
788:     return team.activeEffect !== null && team.activeEffect.type === type;
789:   }
790: }

134: function buildMazeBitmap() {

156:       data[offset + 2] = Math.round(8 + color[2] * 0.32);
157:     } else {
158:       data[offset] = 14;
159:       data[offset + 1] = 12;
160:       data[offset + 2] = 10;

162:     data[offset + 3] = 255;

165:   bitmapCtx.putImageData(image, 0, 0);

188:   };
189: }

209: function frame(now) {
210:   requestAnimationFrame(frame);
211: 
212:   const delta = lastFrame === 0 ? 16 : Math.min(100, now - lastFrame);
213:   lastFrame = now;
214: 
215:   fpsFrames += 1;
216:   fpsTime += delta;
217:   if (fpsTime >= 500) {
218:     fps = (fpsFrames * 1000) / fpsTime;
219:     frameMs = fpsTime / fpsFrames;
220:     fpsFrames = 0;
221:     fpsTime = 0;
222:   }
223: 
224:   if (!document.hidden && game) {
225:     const inputs = bots.inputs();
226:     if (me) {
227:       inputs.set(me.id, playerInput());
228:     }
229: 
230:     const result = game.step(delta, inputs);
231:     handleEvents(result.events);
232:     specialRequested = false;
233:   }
234: 
235:   if (me) {
236:     const follow = me.isGhost ? 0.12 : 0.2;
237:     camera.x += (me.x - camera.x) * follow;
238:     camera.y += (me.y - camera.y) * follow;
239:   }
240: 
241:   render(now);
242:   updateHud(now);
243: }

245: function handleEvents(events) {
246:   for (const event of events) {
247:     switch (event.type) {
248:       case 'bomb':
249:         effects.push({
250:           type: 'blast',
251:           x: event.x,
252:           y: event.y,
253:           radius: event.radius,
254:           life: 620,
255:           total: 620,
256:         });
257:         toast('BOMB — COMMUNAL DEMOLITION', 'bad');
258:         break;
259:       case 'shot':
260:         effects.push({
261:           type: 'flash',
262:           x: event.x ?? null,
263:           y: event.y ?? null,
264:           playerId: event.playerId,
265:           life: 90,
266:           total: 90,
267:         });
268:         break;
269:       case 'item-collected': {
270:         const meta = ITEM_META[event.itemType];
271:         if (event.duplicate) {
272:           toast(`${meta.name} — ALREADY HELD`);
273:         } else if (event.immediate) {
274:           toast(`${meta.name} ADDED TO TEAM RESERVES`, 'good');
275:         } else {
276:           toast(`${meta.name} — PRESS SPACE TO SHARE`, 'good');
277:         }
278:         break;
279:       }
280:       case 'special-used': {
281:         const meta = ITEM_META[event.type];
282:         toast(`${meta.name} TRIGGERED FOR THE TEAM`, 'good');
283:         break;
284:       }
285:       case 'team-downed': {
286:         const team = game.teams[event.teamId];
287:         if (event.teamId === me.teamId) {
288:           toast('TEAM DOWN — HALF WILL RETURN', 'bad');
289:         } else {
290:           toast(`${team.name.toUpperCase()} DOWN`, 'good');
291:         }
292:         break;
293:       }
294:       case 'team-revived': {
295:         const team = game.teams[event.teamId];
296:         if (event.teamId === me.teamId) {
297:           toast(`REVIVED ${event.living} COMRADES — ${event.hp} HP`, 'good');
298:         } else {
299:           toast(`${team.name.toUpperCase()} REVIVED ${event.living}`, 'bad');
300:         }
301:         break;
302:       }
303:       case 'team-eliminated': {
304:         const team = game.teams[event.teamId];
305:         if (event.teamId === me.teamId) {
306:           showBanner('ELIMINATED', 'Press R for a new match');
307:         } else {
308:           toast(`${team.name.toUpperCase()} ELIMINATED`, 'good');
309:         }
310:         break;
311:       }
312:       case 'effect-ended':
313:         if (event.teamId === me.teamId) {
314:           toast(`${EFFECT_NAMES[event.effect] ?? event.effect} EXPIRED`);
315:         }
316:         break;
317:       default:
318:         break;
319:     }
320:   }
321: }

329: function toast(message, tone = '') {

332:   element.textContent = message;

335:   while (ui.toasts.children.length > 4) {

340: }

364: function drawTiles() {
365:   const halfWidth = viewWidth / (2 * TILE_PX) + 1;
366:   const halfHeight = viewHeight / (2 * TILE_PX) + 1;
367:   const minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camera.x - halfWidth));
368:   const maxX = Math.min(game.maze.width - 1, Math.ceil(camera.x + halfWidth));
369:   const minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(camera.y - halfHeight));
370:   const maxY = Math.min(game.maze.height - 1, Math.ceil(camera.y + halfHeight));
371: 
372:   const wallShades = ['#3b3328', '#352e24', '#41382b'];
373: 
374:   for (let y = minY; y <= maxY; y += 1) {
375:     for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x += 1) {
376:       const [screenX, screenY] = worldToScreen(x, y);
377:       const wall = game.maze.tiles[y * game.maze.width + x] === TILE.WALL;
378: 
379:       if (wall) {
380:         const shade = wallShades[(x * 31 + y * 17) % wallShades.length];
381:         ctx.fillStyle = shade;
382:         ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY, TILE_PX + 0.5, TILE_PX + 0.5);
383:         ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.28)';
384:         ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY + TILE_PX - 5, TILE_PX + 0.5, 5);
385:         ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)';
386:         ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY, TILE_PX + 0.5, 2);
387:       } else {
388:         ctx.fillStyle = (x + y) % 2 === 0 ? '#0d0b09' : '#0b0a08';
389:         ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY, TILE_PX + 0.5, TILE_PX + 0.5);
390:         ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.018)';
391:         ctx.fillRect(screenX + TILE_PX - 1, screenY, 1, TILE_PX);
392:         ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY + TILE_PX - 1, TILE_PX, 1);
393:       }
394: 
395:       const owner = spawnTeamAt(game.maze, x, y);
396:       if (owner >= 0 && !wall) {
397:         ctx.fillStyle = withAlpha(game.teams[owner].color, 0.07);
398:         ctx.fillRect(screenX, screenY, TILE_PX + 0.5, TILE_PX + 0.5);
399:       }
400:     }
401:   }
402: }
